U (- x, y, z) = U (x, y, z)\tag{16}
$$

$$
U (x, - y, z) = U (x, y, z)\tag{17}
$$

$$
U (x, y, - z) = U (x, y, z)\tag{18}
$$

显然，式(6)、(11)都满足式(17)和(18).由于有式(15)，故式(6)、(11)也都满足式(16).这就证明了，式(6)、(11)都满足物理上所要求的全部对称性.

二、积分公式问题 前面用积分公式(8)求出了式(11)，也可以用它求出式(6)，为此，须将式(8)化为另一种形式，方法如下：

$$
\left(\sqrt {a s ^ {2} + b s + c} + \sqrt {a} s + \frac {b}{2 \sqrt {a}}\right) \left(\sqrt {a s ^ {2} + b s + c} - \sqrt {a} s - \frac {b}{2 \sqrt {a}}\right) = \frac {4 a c - b ^ {2}}{4 a}\tag{19}
$$

所以

$$
\sqrt {a s ^ {2} + b s + c} + \sqrt {a} s + \frac {b}{2 \sqrt {a}} = \frac {(4 a c - b ^ {2}) / 4 a}{\sqrt {a s ^ {2} + b s + c - \sqrt {a} s - \frac {b}{2 \sqrt {a}}}}\tag{20}
$$

故

$$
\begin{array}{l} \ln \left(\sqrt {a s ^ {2} + b s + c} + \sqrt {a} s + \frac {b}{2 \sqrt {a}}\right) \\ = \ln \left(\frac {4 a c - b ^ {2}}{4 a}\right) - \ln \left(\sqrt {a s ^ {2} + b s + c} - \sqrt {a} s - \frac {b}{2 \sqrt {a}}\right) \end{array}\tag{21}
$$

其中 $\ln \left(\frac{4ac - b^2}{4a}\right)$ 为常数，故式(8)在满足式(9)的条件下，可化为下列积分公式 $\int \frac{ds}{\sqrt{as^2 + bs + c}} = -\frac{1}{\sqrt{a}}\ln \left(\sqrt{as^2 + bs + c} -\sqrt{as} -\frac{b}{2\sqrt{a}}\right) + C''$ （积分常数）

(22) 

把式(22)代入式(3)即得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} U (x, y, z) & = - \frac {q}{8 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} l} \ln \left[ \sqrt {s ^ {2} - 2 x s + x ^ {2} + y ^ {2} + z ^ {2}} - s + x \right] _ {s = - l} ^ {s = l} \\ & = \frac {q}{8 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} l} \ln \left[ \frac {\sqrt {(x + l) ^ {2} + y ^ {2} + z ^ {2}} + x + l}{\sqrt {(x - l) ^ {2} + y ^ {2} + z ^ {2}} + x - l} \right] \end{array}\tag{23}
$$

这便是式(6).

三、 $r=\sqrt{x^{2}+y^{2}+z^{2}}\gg l$ 时的情况 这时式(6)中的根号内 $l^{2}$ 项可去掉，令 $r=\sqrt{x^{2}+y^{2}+z^{2}}$ ，便得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\sqrt {(x + l) ^ {2} + y ^ {2} + z ^ {2}} + x + l}{\sqrt {(x - l) ^ {2} + y ^ {2} + z ^ {2}} + x - l} \approx \frac {\sqrt {r ^ {2} + 2 x l} + x + l}{\sqrt {r ^ {2} - 2 x l} + x - l} \\ = \frac {r \sqrt {1 + \frac {2 x l}{r ^ {2}}} + x + l}{r \sqrt {1 - \frac {2 x l}{r ^ {2}}} + x - l} \approx \frac {r \left(1 + \frac {x l}{r ^ {2}}\right) + x + l}{r \left(1 - \frac {x l}{r ^ {2}}\right) + x - l} \\ = \frac {r + x + l \left(\frac {x}{r} + 1\right)}{r + x - l \left(\frac {x}{r} + 1\right)} = \frac {1 + \frac {l}{r}}{1 - \frac {l}{r}} \end{array}\tag{24}
$$

代入式(6)得 $r \gg l$ 时

$$
U (x, y, z) = \frac {q}{8 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} l} \ln \frac {1 + \frac {l}{r}}{1 - \frac {l}{r}}\tag{25}
$$

利用公式

$$
\ln \left(\frac {1 + x}{1 - x}\right) = 2 \left(x + \frac {1}{3} x ^ {3} + \frac {1}{5} x ^ {5} + \dots\right), (x ^ {2} <   1)\tag{26}
$$

便得

$$
U (x, y, z) = \frac {q}{8 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} l} \cdot 2 \frac {l}{r} = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r}\tag{27}
$$

可见在 $r \gg l$ 处，这段线电荷所产生的电势趋于点电荷产生的电势，正应如此.

1.4.37 电荷量 $q$ 均匀地分布在长为 $2l$ 的一段直线上, 如图 1.4.37(1) 和图 1.4.37(2) 所示, 试求下列各处的电势 $U$ , 并由 $U$ 求电场强度 $\pmb{E}$ : (1) 中垂面上离中心 $O$ 为 $r_1$ 处; (2) 延长线上离 $O$ 为 $r_2$ 处; (3) 端垂面 (通过一端并垂直于直线段的平面) 上离该端为 $r_3$ 处.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-24/e93caaac-7fa3-4e02-8383-9e78eb19a388/84d38875023ce0d9c2375999de576b94a6f4c40c568b36fcc25a801d5b70540b.jpg)



图1.4.37(1)


![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-24/e93caaac-7fa3-4e02-8383-9e78eb19a388/92d550f70daed3c3d2685bdccafb7a2b7b4071c7a1b180db0cf3d0701d5ff6bb.jpg)



图1.4.37(2)


【解】(1)中垂面上离中心 O 为 $r_{1}$ 处,电势为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} U _ {1} & = \frac {q}{8 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} l} \int_ {l} ^ {l} \frac {\mathrm{d} x}{\sqrt {x ^ {2} + r _ {1} ^ {2}}} = \frac {q}{8 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} l} \ln \left(\frac {x + \sqrt {x ^ {2} + r _ {1} ^ {2}}}{r _ {1}}\right) _ {x = - l} ^ {x = l} \\ & = \frac {q}{8 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} l} \ln \left[ \frac {\sqrt {l ^ {2} + r _ {1} ^ {2}} + l}{\sqrt {l ^ {2} + r _ {1} ^ {2}} - l} \right] = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} l} \ln \left[ \frac {\sqrt {r _ {1} ^ {2} + l ^ {2}} + l}{r _ {1}} \right] \end{array}\tag{1}
$$

电场强度 $E_{1}$ 的分量: 因 $U_{1}$ 和 $E_{1}$ 对于 x 轴是对称的, 可以取 $r_{1}=y$ , 于是得

$$
E _ {1 x} = - \frac {\partial U _ {1}}{\partial x} = 0, E _ {1 z} = - \frac {\partial U _ {1}}{\partial z} = 0\tag{2}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} E _ {1 y} & = - \frac {\partial U _ {1}}{\partial y} = - \frac {\partial U _ {1}}{\partial r _ {1}} = - \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} l} \frac {\partial}{\partial r _ {1}} \left[ \ln (\sqrt {r _ {1} ^ {2} + l ^ {2}} + l) - \ln r _ {1} \right] \\ & = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} l} \left[ \frac {1}{r _ {1}} - \frac {r _ {1}}{\sqrt {r _ {1} ^ {2} + l ^ {2}} (\sqrt {r _ {1} ^ {2} + l ^ {2}} + l)} \right] \\ & = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {1}{r _ {1} \sqrt {r _ {1} ^ {2} + l ^ {2}}} \end{array}\tag{3}
$$

故得所求的电场强度为

$$
\boldsymbol {E} = E _ {1 y} \boldsymbol {j} = \frac {q}{4 \pi \epsilon_ {0}} \frac {1}{r _ {1} \sqrt {r _ {1} ^ {2} + l ^ {2}}} \boldsymbol {j} = \frac {q}{4 \pi \epsilon_ {0}} \frac {\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}}{r _ {1} ^ {2} \sqrt {r _ {1} ^ {2} + l ^ {2}}}\tag{4}
$$

式中 $r_{1}=\overrightarrow{OP_{1}}$ .

(2)延长线上离中心为 $r_{2}$ 处,电势为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} U _ {2} & = \frac {q}{8 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} l} \int_ {- l} ^ {l} \frac {\mathrm{d} x}{r _ {2} - x} = - \frac {q}{8 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} l} \ln (r _ {2} - x) \Bigg | _ {x = - l} ^ {x = l} \\ & = \frac {q}{8 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} l} \ln \left(\frac {r _ {2} + l}{r _ {2} - l}\right) \end{array}\tag{5}
$$

电场强度 $E_{2}$ 的分量: 这时

$$
\begin{array}{r l} E _ {2 x} & = - \frac {\partial U _ {2}}{\partial x} = - \frac {\partial U _ {2}}{\partial r _ {2}} = \frac {q}{8 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} l} \frac {\partial}{\partial r _ {2}} \left[ \ln (r _ {2} - l) - \ln (r _ {2} + l) \right] \\ & = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {1}{r _ {2} ^ {2} - l ^ {2}} \\ & E _ {2 y} = - \frac {\partial U _ {2}}{\partial y} = 0, \quad E _ {2 z} = - \frac {\partial U _ {2}}{\partial z} = 0 \end{array}\tag{6}
$$

(7) 

故得所求的电场强度为

$$
\boldsymbol {E} _ {2} = E _ {2, i} \boldsymbol {i} = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}}{r _ {2} (r _ {2} ^ {2} - l ^ {2})}\tag{8}
$$

式中 $r_{2}=OP_{2}$ 

(3)端垂面上离该端为 $r_{3}$ 处,电势为

$$
\begin{array}{l} U _ {3} = \frac {q}{8 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} l} \int_ {- l} ^ {l} \frac {\mathrm{d} x}{\sqrt {(l - x) ^ {2} + r _ {3} ^ {2}}} \\ = - \frac {q}{8 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} l} \ln \left[ \frac {l - x + \sqrt {(l - x) ^ {2} + r _ {3} ^ {2}}}{r _ {3}} \right] _ {x = - l} ^ {x = l} \\ = \frac {q}{8 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} l} \ln \left[ \frac {\sqrt {r _ {3} ^ {2} + 4 l ^ {2}} + 2 l}{r _ {3}} \right] \end{array}\tag{9}
$$

这个形式的 $U_{3}$ 不能用来求 $\pmb{E}_{3}$ 的 $x$ 分量. 因为在积分后, $P_{3}$ 点的横坐标 $l$ 已与带电线的半长度 $l$ 合而为一, 故无法仅对 $P_{3}$ 的横坐标 $l$ 求导. 为了解决问题, 我们先求一个横坐标为 $a$ 的点 $P(a, r_{3}, 0)$ 的电势 $U(a, r_{3}, 0)$ , 再对 $a$ 求导, 然后令 $a = l$ , 即可得出 $\pmb{E}_{3}$ 的 $x$ 分量来.

如图1.4.37(3)， $P(a,r_3,0)$ 点的电势为

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-24/e93caaac-7fa3-4e02-8383-9e78eb19a388/a7d7c65868f2c3c0c2cb45783c639009097c4863419efe4dac58c0c40ca26d7f.jpg)



图1.4.37(3)


$$
\begin{array}{l} U (a, r _ {3}, 0) = \frac {q}{8 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} l} \int_ {- l} ^ {l} \frac {\mathrm{d} x}{\sqrt {(a - x) ^ {2} + r _ {3} ^ {2}}} \\ = - \frac {q}{8 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} l} \ln \left[ \frac {a - x + \sqrt {(a - x) ^ {2} + r _ {3} ^ {2}}}{r _ {3}} \right] _ {x = - l} ^ {x = l} \\ = \frac {q}{8 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} l} \ln \left[ \frac {a + l + \sqrt {(a + l) ^ {2} + r _ {3} ^ {2}}}{a - l + \sqrt {(a - l) ^ {2} + r _ {3} ^ {2}}} \right] \end{array}\tag{10}
$$

由于

$$
\frac {\partial}{\partial a} \ln \left[ \frac {a + l + \sqrt {(a + l) ^ {2} + r _ {3} ^ {2}}}{a - l + \sqrt {(a - l) ^ {2} + r _ {3} ^ {2}}} \right]
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} = \frac {\partial}{\partial a} \left\{\ln \left[ a + l + \sqrt {(a + l) ^ {2} + r _ {3} ^ {2}} \right] - \ln \left[ a - l + \sqrt {(a - l) ^ {2} + r _ {3} ^ {2}} \right] \right\} \\ = \frac {1}{a + l + \sqrt {(a + l) ^ {2} + r _ {3} ^ {2}}} \left[ 1 + \frac {a + l}{\sqrt {(a + l) ^ {2} + r _ {3} ^ {2}}} \right] \\ - \frac {1}{a - l + \sqrt {(a - l) ^ {2} + r _ {3} ^ {2}}} \left[ 1 + \frac {a - l}{\sqrt {(a - l) ^ {2} + r _ {3} ^ {2}}} \right] \\ = \frac {1}{\sqrt {(a + l) ^ {2} + r _ {3} ^ {2}}} - \frac {1}{\sqrt {(a - l) ^ {2} + r _ {3} ^ {2}}} \end{array}\tag{11}
$$

故得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} E _ {3 x} & = - \frac {\partial U}{\partial a} \Bigg | _ {a = l} = \frac {q}{8 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} l} \left[ \frac {1}{\sqrt {(a - l) ^ {2} + r _ {3} ^ {2}}} - \frac {1}{\sqrt {(a + l) ^ {2} + r _ {3} ^ {2}}} \right] _ {a = l} \\ & = \frac {q}{8 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} l} \left[ \frac {1}{r _ {3}} - \frac {1}{\sqrt {r _ {3} ^ {2} + 4 l ^ {2}}} \right] \end{array}\tag{12}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} E _ {3 y} & = - \frac {\partial U _ {3}}{\partial y} = \frac {q}{8 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} l} \frac {\partial}{\partial r _ {3}} \left[ \ln r _ {3} - \ln \left(\sqrt {r _ {3} ^ {2} + 4 l ^ {2}} + 2 l\right) \right] \\ & = \frac {q}{8 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} l} \left[ \frac {1}{r _ {3}} - \frac {1}{\sqrt {r _ {3} ^ {2} + 4 l ^ {2}} + 2 l} \frac {r _ {3}}{\sqrt {r _ {3} ^ {2} + 4 l ^ {2}}} \right] \\ & = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {1}{r _ {3} \sqrt {r _ {3} ^ {2} + 4 l ^ {2}}} \end{array}\tag{13}
$$

$$
E _ {3 z} = - \frac {\partial U _ {3}}{\partial z} = 0\tag{14}
$$

故得所求的电场强度为

$$
\boldsymbol {E} _ {3} = E _ {3 x} \boldsymbol {i} + E _ {3 y} \boldsymbol {j} = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \left[ \frac {1}{2 l} \left(\frac {1}{r _ {3}} - \frac {1}{\sqrt {r _ {3} ^ {2} + 4 l ^ {2}}}\right) \boldsymbol {i} + \frac {1}{r _ {3} \sqrt {r _ {3} ^ {2} + 4 l ^ {2}}} \boldsymbol {j} \right]\tag{15}
$$

1.4.38 一段直线均匀带电,试证明:其等势面是以该直线两端为焦点、并以该直线为轴的旋转椭球面.

【证】设这段直线长为 $2l$ ，电荷量 $q$ 均匀分布在它上面；以这段直线的中点为原点，取笛卡儿坐标系，使这段直线与 $x$ 轴重合，则由前面1.4.36题的结果式(6)，这段直线外空间任一点 $P(x, y, z)$ 的电势为

$$
U (x, y, z) = \frac {q}{8 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} l} \ln \left[ \frac {\sqrt {(x + l) ^ {2} + y ^ {2} + z ^ {2}} + x + l}{\sqrt {(x - l) ^ {2} + y ^ {2} + z ^ {2}} + x - l} \right]\tag{1}
$$

以带电线 $(x$ 轴)为旋转轴，以 $(-l,0,0)$ 点和 $(l,0,0)$ 点为焦点的旋转椭球面为

$$
\frac {x ^ {2}}{a ^ {2}} + \frac {y ^ {2} + z ^ {2}}{b ^ {2}} = 1\tag{2}
$$

因

$$
c ^ {2} = a ^ {2} - b ^ {2} = l ^ {2}\tag{3}
$$

故由式(2)得

(4) 

所以

$$
\begin{array}{r l} y ^ {2} + z ^ {2} & = b ^ {2} - \frac {b ^ {2}}{a ^ {2}} x ^ {2} = a ^ {2} - l ^ {2} - \frac {a ^ {2} - l ^ {2}}{a ^ {2}} x ^ {2} \\ & = a ^ {2} - l ^ {2} - x ^ {2} + \frac {l ^ {2}}{a ^ {2}} x ^ {2} \\ & \sqrt {(x \pm l) ^ {2} + y ^ {2} + z ^ {2}} + x \pm l \\ & = \sqrt {x ^ {2} \pm 2 x l + l ^ {2} + a ^ {2} - l ^ {2} - x ^ {2} + \frac {l ^ {2}}{a ^ {2}} x ^ {2}} + x \pm l \\ & = \sqrt {a ^ {2} + \frac {l ^ {2}}{a ^ {2}} x ^ {2} \pm 2 x l} + x \pm l \\ & = a \pm \frac {l}{a} x + x \pm l \\ & = a + x \pm l \left(\frac {x}{a} + 1\right) \\ & = \frac {1}{a} (a + x) (a \pm l) \end{array}\tag{5}
$$

代入式(1)便得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} U (x, y, z) & = \frac {q}{8 \pi \epsilon_ {0} l} \mathrm{ln} \Big [ \frac {(a + x) (a + l)}{(a + x) (a - l)} \Big ] \\ & = \frac {q}{8 \pi \epsilon_ {0} l} \mathrm{ln} \Big [ \frac {a + l}{a - l} \Big ] \\ & = \text {常数} \end{array}\tag{6}
$$

可见以带电线为轴线,它的两端为焦点的旋转椭球面式(2)是等势面.

【讨论】在式(5)的计算过程中,我们取了

$$
\sqrt {a ^ {2} - 2 l x + \frac {l ^ {2}}{a ^ {2}} x ^ {2}} = \sqrt {\left(a - \frac {l}{a} x\right) ^ {2}} = a - \frac {l}{a} x\tag{7}
$$

若取

$$
\sqrt {a ^ {2} - 2 l x + \frac {l _ {2}}{a ^ {2}} x ^ {2}} = \sqrt {\left(a - \frac {l}{a} x\right) ^ {2}} = \frac {l}{a} x - a\tag{8}
$$

便得出

$$
\begin{array}{r l} U (x, y, z) & = \frac {q}{8 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} l} \ln \left[ \frac {\sqrt {(x + l) ^ {2} + y ^ {2} + z ^ {2}} + x + l}{\sqrt {(x - l) ^ {2} + y ^ {2} + z ^ {2}} + x - l} \right] \\ & = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} l} \ln \left[ \frac {a + \frac {l}{a} x + x + l}{\frac {l}{a} x - a + x - l} \right] \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} = \frac {q}{8 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} l} \ln \left[ \frac {a ^ {2} + l x + a x + a l}{l x - a ^ {2} + a x - a l} \right] \\ = \frac {q}{8 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} l} \ln \left[ \frac {(a + l) (a + x)}{(a + l) (x - a)} \right] \\ = \frac {q}{8 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} l} \ln \left[ \frac {a + x}{x - a} \right] \end{array}\tag{9}
$$

这个 $U$ 便不是常数, 而是 $x$ 的函数. 为什么不能用式(8)呢? 这是因为, 根据式(2), 椭球面上的 $|x| < a$ , 故 $a + x > 0, x - a < 0$ , 这时 $\ln \left[\frac{a + x}{x - a}\right]$ 便无意义, 故不能用式(8).

1.4.39 一条无穷长直线均匀带电,单位长度的电荷量为 $\lambda$ .试求离这直线分

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-24/e93caaac-7fa3-4e02-8383-9e78eb19a388/d77f23c74a5bb8e18b3fe89f622beaecd0d3cf7ab371d3874d7916c3727080a6.jpg)



图1.4.39


别为 $r_{1}$ 和 $r_{2}$ 的两点的电势差.

【解】以带电线为 x 轴，P 为空间任一点，到该线的距离为 r，如图 1.4.39 所示。线上 dx 段的电荷量 $dq = \lambda dx$ 在 P 点产生的电势为

$$
\mathrm{d} U = \frac {\lambda \mathrm{d} x}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} \sqrt {x ^ {2} + r ^ {2}}}\tag{1}
$$

积分便得 P 点的电势为

$$
\begin{array}{l}U = \frac {\lambda}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \frac {\mathrm{d} x}{\sqrt {x ^ {2} + r ^ {2}}} = \frac {\lambda}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \ln (\sqrt {x ^ {2} + r ^ {2}} + x) \Bigg | _ {x = - \infty} ^ {x = - \infty}\\= \frac {\lambda}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \lim _ {x \rightarrow \infty} \ln \left[ \frac {\sqrt {x ^ {2} + r ^ {2}} + x}{\sqrt {x ^ {2} + r ^ {2}} - x} \right]\end{array}\tag{2}
$$

由式(2)得离该直线分别为 $r_{1}$ 和 $r_{2}$ 的两点的电势差为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} U _ {1} - U _ {2} & = \frac {\lambda}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \lim _ {x \to \infty} \left[ \ln \frac {\sqrt {x ^ {2} + r _ {1} ^ {2}} + x}{\sqrt {x ^ {2} + r _ {1} ^ {2}} - x} - \ln \frac {\sqrt {x ^ {2} + r _ {2} ^ {2}} + x}{\sqrt {x ^ {2} + r _ {2} ^ {2}} - x} \right] \\ & = \frac {\lambda}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \lim _ {x \to \infty} \ln \left[ \frac {\sqrt {x ^ {2} + r _ {1} ^ {2}} + x}{\sqrt {x ^ {2} + r _ {1} ^ {2}} - x} \frac {\sqrt {x ^ {2} + r _ {2} ^ {2}} - x}{\sqrt {x ^ {2} + r _ {2} ^ {2}} + x} \right] \\ & = \frac {\lambda}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \lim _ {x \to \infty} \ln \left[ \frac {\sqrt {1 + \left(\frac {r _ {1}}{x}\right) ^ {2}} + 1}{\sqrt {1 + \left(\frac {r _ {2}}{x}\right) ^ {2}} + 1} \frac {\sqrt {1 + \left(\frac {r _ {2}}{x}\right) ^ {2}} - 1}{\sqrt {1 + \left(\frac {r _ {1}}{x}\right) ^ {2}} - 1} \right] \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l}= \frac {\lambda}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \lim _ {x \rightarrow \infty} \ln \left[ \frac {\sqrt {1 + \left(\frac {r _ {2}}{x}\right) ^ {2}} - 1}{\sqrt {1 + \left(\frac {r _ {1}}{x}\right) ^ {2}} - 1} \right]\\= \frac {\lambda}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \lim _ {x \rightarrow \infty} \ln \left[ \frac {1 + \frac {1}{2} \left(\frac {r _ {2}}{x}\right) ^ {2} - 1}{1 + \frac {1}{2} \left(\frac {r _ {1}}{x}\right) ^ {2} - 1} \right]\\= \frac {\lambda}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \lim _ {x \rightarrow \infty} \ln \frac {\frac {1}{2} \left(\frac {r _ {2}}{x}\right) ^ {2}}{\frac {1}{2} \left(\frac {r _ {1}}{x}\right) ^ {2}}\\= \frac {\lambda}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \ln \left(\frac {r _ {2}}{r _ {1}}\right) ^ {2}\\= \frac {\lambda}{2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \ln \frac {r _ {2}}{r _ {1}}\end{array}\tag{3}
$$

【别解】根据前面1.2.13题的式(7)或1.3.19题的式(1)，无穷长均匀直线电荷的电场强度为

$$
\boldsymbol {E} = \frac {\lambda}{2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {\boldsymbol {r}}{r ^ {2}}.\tag{4}
$$

故所求的电势差为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} U _ {1} - U _ {2} & = \int_ {1} ^ {2} \boldsymbol {E} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {l} = \frac {\lambda}{2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \int_ {1} ^ {2} \frac {\boldsymbol {r} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {r}}{r ^ {2}} \\ & - \frac {\lambda}{2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \int_ {1} ^ {2} \frac {\mathrm{d} r}{r} = \frac {\lambda}{2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \ln \frac {r _ {2}}{r _ {1}} \end{array}\tag{5}
$$

【讨论】无穷长均匀带电直线的电势问题

前面式(2)中的极限为

$$
\lim _ {x \rightarrow \infty} \ln \left[ \frac {\sqrt {x ^ {2} + r ^ {2}} + x}{\sqrt {x ^ {2} + r ^ {2}} - x} \right] = \lim _ {x \rightarrow \infty} \ln \left[ \frac {\sqrt {1 + \left(\frac {r}{x}\right) ^ {2}} + 1}{\sqrt {1 + \left(\frac {r}{x}\right) ^ {2}} - 1} \right]\rightarrow \infty\tag{6}
$$

这表明,无穷长均匀带电直线所产生的电势为无穷大.每一点的电势都是无穷大,这是没有意义的.之所以出现这种情况,是由于规定了无穷远处电势为零.如果不规定无穷远处电势为零,而规定某个地方电势为零,就不会出现上述情况.例如,规定离带电线为R处电势为零,则由式(4)得出,离带电线为r处的电势为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} U & = \int_ {r} ^ {R} \boldsymbol {E} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {l} = \frac {\lambda}{2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \int_ {r} ^ {R} \frac {\boldsymbol {r} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {r}}{r ^ {2}} \\ & = \frac {\lambda}{2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \int_ {r} ^ {R} \frac {\mathrm{d} r}{r} = \frac {\lambda}{2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \ln \frac {R}{r} \end{array}\tag{7}
$$

这样,每点的电势就都有意义了.

既然式(2)没有意义,为什么还能用它算出有意义的式(3)来呢?这是因为某点电势的值受人为规定电势零点的影响,零点不同,电势的值便不同.但任何两点电势差的值却与电势零点无关,因为在相减时去掉了电势零点的影响.所以我们可以用式(2)的形式,求出正确的式(3)来.

在物理上,有意义的是电势差,而不是电势.

1.4.40 两条都均匀带电的无穷长平行直线,单位长度的电荷量分别为 $\lambda$ 和

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-24/e93caaac-7fa3-4e02-8383-9e78eb19a388/1e81d31da4af66bf8b067b457ede0a118482448657d6e8f512ad5035c9b53780.jpg)


- $\lambda$ ，相距为 $2a$ ，如图1.4.40所示（图中两带电直线都与纸面垂直），试求空间任一点 $P(x,y)$ 的电势.

【解】z轴(与纸面垂直,图1.4.40中未画出)上坐标为z处,两带电线上的电荷元 $\lambda dz$ 和 $-\lambda dz$ 在 $P(x,y)$ 点产生的电势为

图1.4.40

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \mathrm{d} U & = \mathrm{d} U _ {+} + \mathrm{d} U _ {-} \\ & = \frac {\lambda \mathrm{d} z}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} \sqrt {(x - a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2} + z ^ {2}}} + \frac {- \lambda \mathrm{d} z}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} \sqrt {(x + a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2} + z ^ {2}}} \\ & = \frac {\lambda}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \left[ \frac {\mathrm{d} z}{\sqrt {(x - a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2} + z ^ {2}}} - \frac {\mathrm{d} z}{\sqrt {(x + a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2} + z ^ {2}}} \right] \end{array}\tag{1}
$$

积分便得 $P(x,y)$ 点的电势为

$$
\begin{array}{r l}U&= \frac {\lambda}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \left[ \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \frac {\mathrm{d} z}{\sqrt {(x - a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2} + z ^ {2}}} - \int_ {- \infty} ^ {\infty} \frac {\mathrm{d} z}{\sqrt {(x + a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2} + z ^ {2}}} \right]\\&= \frac {\lambda}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \lim _ {z \rightarrow \infty} \left\{\ln \left[ \sqrt {z ^ {2} + (x - a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2}} + z \right] _ {- z} ^ {z} - \ln \left[ \sqrt {z ^ {2} + (x + a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2}} + z \right] _ {- z} ^ {z} \right\}\\&= \frac {\lambda}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \lim _ {z \rightarrow \infty} \ln \left[ \frac {\sqrt {z ^ {2} + (x - a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2}} + z}{\sqrt {z ^ {2} + (x - a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2}} - z} \times \frac {\sqrt {z ^ {2} + (x + a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2}} - z}{\sqrt {z ^ {2} + (x + a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2}} + z} \right]\\&= \frac {\lambda}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \lim _ {z \rightarrow \infty} \ln \left[ \frac {\sqrt {1 + \frac {(x - a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2}}{z ^ {2}}} + 1}{\sqrt {1 + \frac {(x - a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2}}{z ^ {2}}} - 1} \times \frac {\sqrt {1 + \frac {(x + a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2}}{z ^ {2}}} - 1}{\sqrt {1 + \frac {(x + a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2}}{z ^ {2}}} + 1} \right]\end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l}= \frac {\lambda}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \lim _ {z \rightarrow \infty} \ln \left[ \frac {1 + \frac {1}{2} \frac {(x - a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2}}{z ^ {2}} + 1}{1 + \frac {1}{2} \frac {(x - a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2}}{z ^ {2}} - 1} \times \frac {1 + \frac {1}{2} \frac {(x + a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2}}{z ^ {2}} - 1}{1 + \frac {1}{2} \frac {(x + a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2}}{z ^ {2}} + 1} \right]\\= \frac {\lambda}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \ln \left[ \frac {(x + a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2}}{(x - a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2}} \right]\end{array}\tag{2}
$$

【别解】规定图1.4.40中 $z$ 轴上的电势为零，则由前面1.4.39题的式(7)， $P(x,y)$ 点的电势为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} U & = U _ {+} + U _ {-} = \frac {\lambda}{2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \ln \left[ \frac {a}{\sqrt {(x - a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2}}} \right] + \frac {- \lambda}{2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \ln \left[ \frac {a}{\sqrt {(x + a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2}}} \right] \\ & = \frac {\lambda}{2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \ln \sqrt {\frac {(x + a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2}}{(x - a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2}}} \\ & = \frac {\lambda}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \ln \left[ \frac {(x + a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2}}{(x - a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2}} \right] \end{array}\tag{3}
$$

【讨论】以无穷远处为电势零点,一条无穷长的均匀带电直线所产生的电势为无穷大,但两条无穷长的均匀带电直线(单位长度的电荷量分别为 $\lambda$ 和 $-\lambda$ ) 所产生的电势却为有限值.这是因为,尽管两条带电线中的每一条所产生的电势都是无穷大,但由于它们单位长度的电荷量大小相等而符号相反,结果它们的电势在相加时,便消去了无穷大,而成为有限值,式(2)便是这样来的.

1.4.41 两条均匀带电的无穷长平行直线, 相距为 $2a$ , 单位长度的电荷量分别为 $\lambda$ 和 $-\lambda$ ; 以两带电线构成的平面为 $z - x$ 平面, 使 $z$ 轴与两线平行且距离相等, 取笛卡儿坐标系如图 1.4.41(1) 所示. 试证明: (1) 电势为 $U$ 的等势面是半径为 $r = \left|\frac{2ka}{k^2 - 1}\right|$ 的圆柱面, 其中 $k = \mathrm{e}^{2\pi \epsilon_0 U / \lambda}$ , 圆柱的轴线与两带电的直线平行且共面, 位置在 $x$ 轴上 $x_0 = \frac{k^2 + 1}{k^2 - 1} a$ 处; (2) 在 $x - y$ 平面内, 电场线的方程为 $x^2 + y^2 -$ $by - a^{2} = 0$ , 即圆心在 y 轴上的圆, 其中 b 为常数.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-24/e93caaac-7fa3-4e02-8383-9e78eb19a388/0d101428ac0d6f577b1065da1fa3dff704bc8687b7020f805941fa4fe167458a.jpg)



图1.4.41(1)


【证】(1)如图1.4.41(1)， $P(x,y,z)$ 点的电势为[参见前1.4.40题的式(2)或式(3)]

$$
U = \frac {\lambda}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \ln \left[ \frac {(x + a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2}}{(x - a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2}} \right]\tag{1}
$$

由于对称性， $U$ 与 $\pmb{z}$ 无关.为方便，令

$$
k = \mathrm{e} ^ {\frac {2 \pi \epsilon_ {0} U}{\lambda}}\tag{2}
$$

则得

$$
\frac {(x + a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2}}{(x - a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2}} = k ^ {2}\tag{3}
$$

此式可化为

$$
\left(x - \frac {k ^ {2} + 1}{k ^ {2} - 1} a\right) ^ {2} + y ^ {2} = \left(\frac {2 k a}{k ^ {2} - 1}\right) ^ {2}\tag{4}
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-24/e93caaac-7fa3-4e02-8383-9e78eb19a388/958ed498fab244a7fc28e0a9a2aa58ed3091d92f361b31d904fd48507e3a5c83.jpg)



图1.4.41(2)


在三维空间,这是一个圆柱面,它的轴线在 z-x 平面内并与 z 轴平行,位置在 $x=\frac{k^{2}-1}{k^{2}+1}a$ 处,它的半径为 $r=\left|\frac{2ka}{k^{2}-1}\right|$ .

(2) 在 $x - y$ 平面内, 如图 1.4.41(2), $P(x, y)$ 点的电场强度为

$$
\boldsymbol {E} = \boldsymbol {E}, + \boldsymbol {E} _ {-}\tag{5}
$$

根据无穷长均匀直线电荷的电场强度的公式[参见前1.2.13题的式(7)或1.3.19题的式(1)]， $\pmb{E}$ 的两个分量分别为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} E _ {x} & = E _ {+ x} + E _ {- x} \\ & = \frac {\lambda}{2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {1}{\sqrt {(x - a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2}}} \frac {x - a}{\sqrt {(x - a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2}}} \\ & \quad + \frac {- \lambda}{2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {1}{\sqrt {(x + a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2}}} \frac {x + a}{\sqrt {(x + a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2}}} \\ & = \frac {\lambda}{2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \left[ \frac {x - a}{(x - a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2}} - \frac {x + a}{(x + a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2}} \right] \\ E _ {y} & = E _ {+ y} + E _ {- y} \\ & = \frac {\lambda}{2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {1}{\sqrt {(x - a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2}}} \frac {y}{\sqrt {(x - a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2}}} \\ & \quad + \frac {- \lambda}{2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {1}{\sqrt {(x + a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2}}} \frac {y}{\sqrt {(x + a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2}}} \\ & = \frac {\lambda y}{2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \left[ \frac {1}{(x - a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2}} - \frac {1}{(x + a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2}} \right] \end{array}\tag{6}
$$

(7) 

电场强度 E 的方向就是电场线的切线方向, 故电场线的微分方程为

所以

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \frac {\mathrm{d} y}{\mathrm{d} x} & = \frac {E _ {y}}{E _ {x}} = \frac {\frac {y}{(x - a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2}} - \frac {y}{(x + a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2}}}{\frac {x - a}{(x - a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2}} - \frac {x + a}{(x + a) ^ {2} + y ^ {2}}} \\ & = \frac {2 x y}{x ^ {2} - y ^ {2} - a ^ {2}} \\ & 2 x y \mathrm{d} x - (x ^ {2} - y ^ {2} - a ^ {2}) \mathrm{d} y = 0 \\ & 2 x y \mathrm{d} x - x ^ {2} \mathrm{d} y + (y ^ {2} + a ^ {2}) \mathrm{d} y = 0 \end{array}\tag{8}
$$

(9) 

以 $\frac{1}{y^2}$ 乘式(9)得

所以

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & \frac {2 x y \mathrm{d} x - x ^ {2} \mathrm{d} y}{y ^ {2}} + \left(1 + \frac {a ^ {2}}{y ^ {2}}\right) \mathrm{d} y = 0 \\ & \mathrm{d} \left(\frac {x ^ {2}}{y}\right) + \mathrm{d} y - a ^ {2} \mathrm{d} \left(\frac {1}{y}\right) = 0 \\ & \mathrm{d} \left(\frac {x ^ {2}}{y} + y - \frac {a ^ {2}}{y}\right) = 0 \end{array}\tag{10}
$$

积分得

$$
\frac {x ^ {2}}{y} + y - \frac {a ^ {2}}{y} = b (\text {常数})\tag{11}
$$

所以

$$
x ^ {2} + y ^ {2} - b y - a ^ {2} = 0\tag{12}
$$

1.4.42 电荷量 q 均匀分布在长为 4l 的一段直线上，在电荷分布不变的情况下，把这段直线弯成边长为 l 的正方形。(1)试求这正方形轴线上离中心为 r 处的电势；(2)试证明：在 $r \gg l$ 处，这电势相当于点电荷 q 产生的电势。

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-24/e93caaac-7fa3-4e02-8383-9e78eb19a388/530455e1f2651c2a5d7da1e2fd83cfc266f3621904902c50546c492a433e86f6.jpg)


【解】（1）如图1.4.42， $P$ 点的电势为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} U & = 4 \frac {1}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {q}{4 l} \int_ {- l / 2} ^ {l / 2} \frac {\mathrm{d} x}{\sqrt {r ^ {2} + l ^ {2} / 4 + x ^ {2}}} \\ & = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} l} \ln \left[ \sqrt {r ^ {2} + l ^ {2} / 4 + x ^ {2}} + x \right] _ {x = - l / 2} ^ {x = l / 2} \\ & = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} l} \ln \left[ \frac {\sqrt {r ^ {2} + l ^ {2} / 2} + l / 2}{\sqrt {r ^ {2} + l ^ {2} / 2} - l / 2} \right] \end{array}
$$

图1.4.42

(1) 

此式也可以化成

$$
U = \frac {q}{2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} l} \ln \left[ \frac {\sqrt {r ^ {2} + l ^ {2} / 2} + l / 2}{\sqrt {r ^ {2} + l ^ {2} / 4}} \right]\tag{2}
$$

(2) 在 $r \gg l$ 处, $\sqrt{r^2 + l^2 / 2} \approx r$ , 故式(1)化为

$$
U \approx \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} l} \ln \frac {r + l / 2}{r - l / 2} = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} l} \ln \left(\frac {1 + l / 2 r}{1 - l / 2 r}\right)\tag{3}
$$

利用公式

$$
\ln \left(\frac {1 + x}{1 - x}\right) = 2 \left(x + \frac {1}{3} x ^ {3} + \frac {1}{5} x ^ {5} + \dots\right), \quad x ^ {2} <   1\tag{4}
$$

便得

$$
U \approx \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} l} \cdot 2 \left(\frac {l}{2 r}\right) = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r}\tag{5}
$$

这便是点电荷 q 在距离为 r 处产生的电势.

1.4.43 电荷量 q 均匀地分布在半径为 R 的圆环上, P 为圆环轴线上离环心

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-24/e93caaac-7fa3-4e02-8383-9e78eb19a388/27d564d7ad9d6a35dad717c24cf575c29bd8e75632b7dbd9e6798790a2019b55.jpg)


为 $r$ 的一点, 如图1.4.43(1)所示. (1)试求 $P$ 点的电势 $U$ , 并由 $U$ 求电场强度 $\pmb{E}$ ; (2)试求 $P$ 点的电场强度 $\pmb{E}$ , 并由 $\pmb{E}$ 求电势 $U$ ; (3)以 $r$ 为横坐标, $U$ 为纵坐标, 画出 $U - r$ 曲线.


图1.4.43(1)


【解】(1) $P$ 点的电势为

$$
U = \int_ {q} \frac {\mathrm{d} q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} \sqrt {r ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}} = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {1}{\sqrt {r ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}}\tag{1}
$$

电场强度为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \boldsymbol {E} & = - \nabla U = - \frac {\partial U}{\partial r} \boldsymbol {e} _ {r} = - \frac {q \boldsymbol {e} _ {r}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \left[ - \frac {1}{2} \frac {2 r}{(r ^ {2} + R ^ {2}) ^ {3 / 2}} \right] \\ & = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {\boldsymbol {r}}{(r ^ {2} + R ^ {2}) ^ {3 / 2}} \end{array}\tag{2}
$$

式中 $r = re_{r} = OP$ 

(2) $P$ 点的电场强度为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \boldsymbol {E} & = \int_ {q} \frac {\mathrm{d} q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} (r ^ {2} + R ^ {2})} \frac {r}{\sqrt {r ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}} \boldsymbol {e} _ {r} \\ & = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {\boldsymbol {r}}{(r ^ {2} + R ^ {2}) ^ {3 / 2}} \end{array}\tag{3}
$$

电势为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} U & = \int_ {r} ^ {\infty} \boldsymbol {E} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {l} = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \int_ {r} ^ {\infty} \frac {\boldsymbol {r} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {l}}{(r ^ {2} + R ^ {2}) ^ {3 / 2}} \\ & = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \int_ {r} ^ {\infty} \frac {r \mathrm{d} r}{(r ^ {2} + R ^ {2}) ^ {3 / 2}} = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \left[ - \frac {1}{\sqrt {r ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}} \right] _ {r} ^ {\infty} \\ & = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {1}{\sqrt {r ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}} \end{array}\tag{4}
$$

(3)由式(1)得

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} U}{\mathrm{d} r} = - \frac {q}{4 \pi \epsilon_ {0}} \frac {r}{(r ^ {2} + R ^ {2}) ^ {3 / 2}}\tag{5}
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} ^ {2} U}{\mathrm{d} r ^ {2}} = - \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {R ^ {2} - 2 r ^ {2}}{(r ^ {2} + R ^ {2}) ^ {5 / 2}}\tag{6}
$$

故知 $r = 0$ 时 $U = \frac{q}{4\pi\varepsilon_0R}$ 为极大值； $U - r$ 曲线在 $r = R / \sqrt{2}$ 处是拐点。依照式(1)画出的 $U - r$ 曲线如图1.4.43(2)所示。

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-24/e93caaac-7fa3-4e02-8383-9e78eb19a388/74c0609cd20095cb0dc7b13836b57e2429589123cfbe1ea37c2c4a988f302921.jpg)



图1.4.43(2)


1.4.44 电荷量 q 均匀分布在半径为 R 的圆环上, 如图 1.4.44(1) 所示. 试证明: 轴线外任一点 $P(r, \theta)$ 的电势 U 的准确表达式是一个椭圆积分.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-24/e93caaac-7fa3-4e02-8383-9e78eb19a388/7e168265de51ed0ce5c829e3d1ac556b4d436449bc3b74234239ce84b57ef639.jpg)



图1.4.44(1)


![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-24/e93caaac-7fa3-4e02-8383-9e78eb19a388/8470cd48882a2c8adffd1d820616198348c7483a07d02622534a7ad0f645fa65.jpg)



图1.4.44(2)


【证】如图1.4.44(2)，过 $P$ 点作直线 $PD$ 平行于轴线，交圆环面于 $D$ 点.以 $OD$ 为方位角 $\phi$ 的起始线，即 $\phi = \angle DOA$ ，则

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \overline {{{A P}}} & = \sqrt {\overline {{{A D}}} ^ {2} + \overline {{{D P}}} ^ {2}} \\ & = \sqrt {R ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta - 2 R r \sin \theta \cos \phi + r ^ {2} \cos^ {2} \theta} \\ & = \sqrt {R ^ {2} + r ^ {2} - 2 R r \sin \theta \cos \phi} \end{array}\tag{1}
$$

A 处的电荷量 $dq=\frac{q}{2\pi R}Rd\phi=\frac{q}{2\pi}d\phi$ 在 P 点产生的电势为

$$
\mathrm{d} U = \frac {\mathrm{d} q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} \overline {{A P}}} = \frac {q}{8 \pi^ {2} \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {\mathrm{d} \phi}{\sqrt {R ^ {2} + r ^ {2} - 2 R r \sin \theta \cos \phi}}\tag{2}
$$

于是得整个圆环电荷在 $P$ 点产生的电势为

$$
\begin{array}{l} U = \frac {q}{8 \pi^ {2} \varepsilon_ {0}} \int_ {0} ^ {2 \pi} \frac {\mathrm{d} \phi}{\sqrt {R ^ {2} + r ^ {2} - 2 R r \sin \theta \cos \phi}} \\ = \frac {q}{4 \pi^ {2} \varepsilon_ {0}} \int_ {0} ^ {\pi} \frac {\mathrm{d} \phi}{\sqrt {R ^ {2} + r ^ {2} - 2 R r \sin \theta \cos \phi}} \end{array}\tag{3}
$$

这是一个椭圆积分,它不能用初等函数的有限项表示出来.

下面我们把式(3)化成用全椭圆积分表示.令

$$
\phi = 2 \alpha - \pi\tag{4}
$$

则 $\mathrm{d}\phi = 2\mathrm{d}\alpha$ 

$$
\cos \phi = \cos (2 \alpha - \pi) = - \cos 2 \alpha = - 1 + 2 \sin^ {2} \alpha\tag{5}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} R ^ {2} + r ^ {2} - 2 R r \sin \theta \cos \phi & = R ^ {2} + r ^ {2} - 2 R r \sin \theta (- 1 + 2 \sin^ {2} \alpha) \\ & = R ^ {2} + r ^ {2} + 2 R r \sin \theta - 4 R r \sin \theta \sin^ {2} \alpha \\ & = (R ^ {2} + r ^ {2} + 2 R r \sin \theta) (1 - k ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \alpha) \end{array}\tag{6}
$$

式中

$$
k ^ {2} = \frac {4 R r \sin \theta}{R ^ {2} + r ^ {2} + 2 R r \sin \theta}\tag{7}
$$

于是得

$$
\begin{array}{l} U = \frac {q}{4 \pi^ {2} \varepsilon_ {0}} \int_ {0} ^ {\pi} \frac {\mathrm{d} \phi}{\sqrt {R ^ {2} + r ^ {2} - 2 R r \sin \theta \cos \phi}} \\ = \frac {q}{4 \pi^ {2} \varepsilon_ {0}} \int_ {\pi / 2} ^ {\pi} \frac {1}{\sqrt {R ^ {2} + r ^ {2} + 2 R r \sin \theta}} \frac {2 \mathrm{d} \alpha}{\sqrt {1 - k ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \alpha}} \\ = \frac {q}{4 \pi^ {2} \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {2}{\sqrt {R ^ {2} + r ^ {2} + 2 R r \sin \theta}} \int_ {\pi / 2} ^ {\pi} \frac {\mathrm{d} \alpha}{\sqrt {1 - k ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \alpha}} \\ = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {2}{\pi \sqrt {R ^ {2} + r ^ {2} + 2 R r \sin \theta}} \int_ {0} ^ {\pi / 2} \frac {\mathrm{d} \alpha}{\sqrt {1 - k ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta}} \end{array}\tag{8}
$$

式中

$$
\begin{array}{l} \int_ {0} ^ {\pi / 2} \frac {\mathrm{d} a}{\sqrt {1 - k ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \alpha}} = K \\ = \frac {\pi}{2} \left[ 1 + \left(\frac {1}{2}\right) ^ {2} k ^ {2} + \left(\frac {1 \cdot 3}{2 \cdot 4}\right) ^ {2} k ^ {4} + \left(\frac {1 \cdot 3 \cdot 5}{2 \cdot 4 \cdot 6}\right) ^ {2} k ^ {6} + \dots \right] \end{array}\tag{9}
$$

称为第一类椭圆积分.

于是得所求的电势为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} U & = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {2}{\sqrt {R ^ {2} + r ^ {2} + 2 R r \sin \theta}} K \\ & = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {1}{\sqrt {R ^ {2} + r ^ {2} + 2 R r \sin \theta}} \times \left[ 1 + \left(\frac {1}{2}\right) ^ {2} k ^ {2} + \left(\frac {1 . 3}{2 . 4}\right) ^ {2} k ^ {4} + \left(\frac {1 \cdot 3 \cdot 5}{2 \cdot 4 \cdot 6}\right) ^ {2} k ^ {6} + \dots \right] \end{array}\tag{10}
$$

当 $\theta=0$ 时，P 点在轴线上，这时 k=0，便得

$$
U = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {1}{\sqrt {R ^ {2} + r ^ {2}}}\tag{11}
$$

这正是前面1.4.43题的式(1).

1.4.45 两个共轴线的均匀带电圆环, 半径都是 R, 相距为 l, 所带电荷量分别为 q 和 -q. 以两环间轴线的中点 O 为原点, 沿轴线取 x 轴, 如图 1.4.45 所示. 已知 $l \ll R$ , 试求轴线上 x 处的电势.

【解】均匀带电圆环在轴线上产生的电势为[参见前1.4.43题的式(1)]

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-24/e93caaac-7fa3-4e02-8383-9e78eb19a388/61915b5fc4b9d626029c2c6f6cc18bc12bf92444cda9562642fabcebdfb75dbd.jpg)


$$
U = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {1}{\sqrt {r ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}}\tag{1}
$$

图1.4.45

由此得出,图 1.4.45 中轴线上 x 处的电势为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} U & = U _ {1} + U _ {-} \\ & = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {1}{\sqrt {(x - l / 2) ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}} + \frac {- q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {1}{\sqrt {(x + l / 2) ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}}; \\ & = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \left[ \frac {1}{\sqrt {(x - l / 2) ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}} - \frac {1}{\sqrt {(x + l / 2) ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}} \right] \end{array}\tag{2}
$$

因 $l \ll R$ ，故上式可以化简，其中

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {1}{\sqrt {(x - l / 2) ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}} - \frac {1}{\sqrt {(x + l / 2) ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}} \\ \approx \frac {1}{\sqrt {x ^ {2} + R ^ {2} - x l}} - \frac {1}{\sqrt {x ^ {2} + R ^ {2} + x l}} \\ = \frac {1}{\sqrt {x ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}} \left[ \left(1 - \frac {x l}{x ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}\right) ^ {- 1 / 2} - \left(1 + \frac {x l}{x ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}\right) ^ {- 1 / 2} \right] \\ \approx \frac {1}{\sqrt {x ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}} \left[ 1 + \frac {1}{2} \frac {x l}{x ^ {2} + R ^ {2}} - 1 + \frac {1}{2} \frac {x l}{x ^ {2} + R ^ {2}} \right] \\ = \frac {x l}{(x ^ {2} + R ^ {2}) ^ {3 / 2}} \end{array}\tag{3}
$$

故得

$$
U = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {l x}{(x ^ {2} + R ^ {2}) ^ {3 / 2}}\tag{4}
$$

因 x 可正可负, 故上式可用于轴线上任何地方.

1.4.46 半径为 R 的圆面上均匀带电, 电荷量的面密度为 $\sigma$ . 试求轴线上离圆心为 r 处的电势 U, 并由 U 求电场强度 E.

【解】以圆心为心、 $x$ 为半径，在圆面上取宽为 $\mathrm{dx}$ 的环带，这环带上的电荷量为

$$
\mathrm{d} q = \sigma \cdot 2 \pi x \mathrm{d} x = 2 \pi \sigma x \mathrm{d} x\tag{1}
$$

dq 在轴线上离圆心为 r 处产生的电势为

$$
\mathrm{d} U = \frac {\mathrm{d} q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {1}{\sqrt {r ^ {2} + x ^ {2}}} = \frac {\sigma}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {x \mathrm{d} x}{\sqrt {r ^ {2} - x ^ {2}}}\tag{2}
$$

积分便得所求的电势为

$$
U = \frac {\sigma}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} \int_ {0} ^ {R} \frac {x \mathrm{d} x}{\sqrt {r ^ {2} + x ^ {2}}} = \frac {\sigma}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} \left(\sqrt {r ^ {2} + R ^ {2}} - r\right)\tag{3}
$$

若给圆面上的电荷量为 q，则 $q = \pi R^{2}\sigma$ ，结果便为

$$
U = \frac {q}{2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \left(\frac {\sqrt {r ^ {2} + R ^ {2}} - r}{R ^ {2}}\right)\tag{4}
$$

所求的电场强度为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \boldsymbol {E} & = - \boldsymbol {\nabla} U = - \frac {\partial U}{\partial r} \boldsymbol {e} _ {r} \\ & = - \frac {q \boldsymbol {e} _ {r}}{2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} R ^ {2}} \left(\frac {r}{\sqrt {r ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}} - 1\right) \\ & = \frac {q}{2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} R ^ {2}} \left(\frac {\boldsymbol {r}}{r} - \frac {\boldsymbol {r}}{\sqrt {r ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}}\right) \end{array}\tag{5}
$$

或用 $\sigma$ 表示为

$$
\boldsymbol {E} = \frac {\sigma}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} \left(\frac {\boldsymbol {r}}{r} - \frac {\boldsymbol {r}}{\sqrt {r ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}}\right)\tag{6}
$$

式中 r 为从圆心到场点的位矢.

1.4.47 两个均匀带电的圆面共轴线, 半径都是 R, 相距为 l, 电荷量的面密度分别为 $\sigma$ 和 $-\sigma$ . 以它们间轴线的中点为原点 O, 沿轴线取 x 轴, 如图 1.4.47 (1) 所示. 已知 $l \ll R$ , 试求轴线上 x 处的 (1) 电场强度 E 和 (2) 电势 U.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-24/e93caaac-7fa3-4e02-8383-9e78eb19a388/49cae1d8a25deffe70cd36e3c120b9c1fcf137736fa27895037891ad1448454e.jpg)



图1.4.47(1)


![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-24/e93caaac-7fa3-4e02-8383-9e78eb19a388/33d46517e9532bf38a4c3d8823b04997589e6e82ab5f6290c9d7d8f917e0814a.jpg)



图1.4.47(2)


【解】（1）根据前面1.4.46题的式(6)，均匀带电的圆面在轴线上离圆心为 $r$ 处产生的电场强度为

$$
\boldsymbol {E} = \frac {\sigma}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} \left(1 - \frac {r}{\sqrt {r ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}}\right) \boldsymbol {e} _ {r}\tag{1}
$$

式中 $e_{r}=r/r$ 是沿轴线向外的单位矢量. 如图 1.4.47(2)，这两个圆面电荷在轴线上 x>1/2 处产生的电场强度为

$$
\begin{array}{l} \boldsymbol {E} = \boldsymbol {E} _ {+} + \boldsymbol {E} _ {-} \\ = \frac {\sigma}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} \left(1 - \frac {x - l / 2}{\sqrt {(x - l / 2) ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}}\right) \boldsymbol {e} _ {x} + \frac {- \sigma}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} \left(1 - \frac {x + l / 2}{\sqrt {(x + l / 2) ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}}\right) \boldsymbol {e} _ {x} \\ = \frac {\sigma}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} \left[ \frac {x + l / 2}{\sqrt {(x + l / 2) ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}} - \frac {x - l / 2}{\sqrt {(x - l / 2) ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}} \right] \boldsymbol {e} _ {x} \end{array}\tag{2}
$$

式中 $e_{x}$ 为 x 轴方向上的单位矢量.

因 $l \ll R$ ，故式(2)方括号内可简化如下：

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\frac {x + l / 2}{\sqrt {(x + l / 2) ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}} - \frac {x - l / 2}{\sqrt {(x - l / 2) ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}}} \\ & {\approx \frac {x + l / 2}{\sqrt {x ^ {2} + x l + R ^ {2}}} - \frac {x - l / 2}{\sqrt {x ^ {2} - x l + R ^ {2}}}} \\ & {= \frac {1}{\sqrt {x ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}} \bigg [ (x + l / 2) \left(1 + \frac {x l}{x ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}\right) ^ {- 1 / 2} - (x - l / 2) \left(1 - \frac {x l}{x ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}\right) ^ {- 1 / 2} \bigg ]} \\ & {\approx \frac {1}{\sqrt {x ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}} \bigg [ (x + l / 2) \left(1 - \frac {1}{2} \frac {x l}{x ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}\right) - (x - l / 2) \left(1 + \frac {1}{2} \frac {x l}{x ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}\right) \bigg ]} \\ & {= \frac {1}{\sqrt {- x ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}} \bigg [ l - \frac {x ^ {2} l}{x ^ {2} + R ^ {2}} \bigg ]} \\ & {= \frac {l R ^ {2}}{(x ^ {2} + R ^ {2}) ^ {3 / 2}}} \end{array}\tag{3}
$$

所以

$$
\boldsymbol {E} = \frac {\sigma l R ^ {2}}{2 \varepsilon_ {0} (x ^ {2} + R ^ {2}) ^ {3 / 2}} \boldsymbol {e} _ {x}\tag{4}
$$

在 x<-l/2 处，轴线上 x 处的电场强度为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\pmb {E} = \pmb {E} _ {+} + \pmb {E} _ {-}} \\ & {\qquad = \frac {\sigma}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} \bigg [ 1 + \frac {x - l / 2}{\sqrt {(x - l / 2) ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}} \bigg ] (- \pmb {e} _ {x}) + \frac {- \sigma}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} \bigg [ 1 + \frac {x + l / 2}{\sqrt {(x + l / 2) ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}} \bigg ] (- \pmb {e} _ {x})} \\ & {\qquad = \frac {\sigma}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} \bigg [ \frac {x + l / 2}{\sqrt {(x + l / 2) ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}} - \frac {x - l / 2}{\sqrt {(x - l / 2) ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}} \bigg ] \pmb {e} _ {x}} \end{array}\tag{5}
$$

式(5)在形式上与 x > l/2 处的式(2)相同, 于是得出: 两圆面之外的电场强度为

$$
\boldsymbol {E} = \frac {\sigma l R ^ {2}}{2 \varepsilon_ {0} (x ^ {2} + R ^ {2}) ^ {3 / 2}} \boldsymbol {e} _ {x}, | x | > l / 2\tag{6}
$$

在两圆面之间， $|x|<l/2$ ，由式(1)，轴线上的电场强度为

$$
\begin{array}{l} \boldsymbol {E} = \boldsymbol {E} _ {+} + \boldsymbol {E} _ {-} \\ = \frac {\sigma}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} \left[ 1 - \frac {l / 2 - x}{\sqrt {(l / 2 - x) ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}} \right] (- \boldsymbol {e} _ {x}) + \frac {- \sigma}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} \left[ 1 - \frac {x + l / 2}{\sqrt {(x + l / 2) ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}} \right] \boldsymbol {e} _ {x} \\ = - \frac {\sigma}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} \left[ 1 + \frac {x - l / 2}{\sqrt {(x - l / 2) ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}} + 1 - \frac {x + l / 2}{\sqrt {(x + l / 2) ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}} \right] \boldsymbol {e} _ {x} \\ = - \frac {\sigma}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} \left[ 2 + \frac {x - l / 2}{\sqrt {(x - l / 2) ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}} - \frac {x + l / 2}{\sqrt {(x + l / 2) ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}} \right] \boldsymbol {e} _ {x} \\ \approx - \frac {\sigma}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} \left[ 2 - \frac {l R ^ {2}}{(x ^ {2} + R ^ {2}) ^ {3 / 2}} \right] \boldsymbol {e} _ {x} \end{array}\tag{7}
$$

因 $l \ll R$ ，故得

$$
\boldsymbol {E} \approx - \frac {\sigma}{\epsilon_ {0}} \boldsymbol {e} _ {x}, \quad l / 2 > x > - l / 2\tag{8}
$$

这个结果表明,两圆面间轴线上的电场近似为均匀电场.

(2)根据前面1.4.46题的式(3)，均匀带电的圆面在轴线上离圆心为r处产生的电势为

$$
U = \frac {\sigma}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} \left(\sqrt {r ^ {2} + R ^ {2}} - r\right)\tag{9}
$$

如图 1.4.47(1)，这两个圆面电荷在轴线上 x > l/2 处产生的电势为

$$
U = U _ {+} + U
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} = \frac {\sigma}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} \left[ \sqrt {(x - l / 2) ^ {2} + R ^ {2}} - (x - l / 2) \right] + \frac {- \sigma}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} \left[ \sqrt {(x + l / 2) ^ {2} + R ^ {2}} - (x + l / 2) \right] \\ = \frac {\sigma}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} \left[ \sqrt {(x - l / 2) ^ {2} + R ^ {2}} - x + l / 2 - \sqrt {(x + l / 2) ^ {2} + R ^ {2}} + x + l / 2 \right] \\ = \frac {\sigma}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} \left[ l + \sqrt {(x - l / 2) ^ {2} + R ^ {2}} - \sqrt {(x + l / 2) ^ {2} + R ^ {2}} \right] \end{array}\tag{10}
$$

因 $l \ll R$ ，故上式中两根号项可化简如下：

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sqrt {(x - l / 2) ^ {2} + R ^ {2}} - \sqrt {(x + l / 2) ^ {2} + R ^ {2}} \\ \approx \sqrt {x ^ {2} - x l + R ^ {2}} - \sqrt {x ^ {2} + x l + R ^ {2}} \\ = \sqrt {x ^ {2} + R ^ {2}} \left[ \left(1 - \frac {x l}{x ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}\right) ^ {1 / 2} - \left(1 + \frac {x l}{x ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}\right) ^ {1 / 2} \right] \\ \approx \sqrt {x ^ {2} + R ^ {2}} \left[ 1 - \frac {1}{2} \frac {x l}{x ^ {2} + R ^ {2}} - 1 - \frac {1}{2} \frac {x l}{x ^ {2} + R ^ {2}} \right] \\ = - \frac {x l}{\sqrt {x ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}} \end{array}\tag{11}
$$

故得

$$
U = \frac {\sigma}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} \left[ l - \frac {x l}{\sqrt {x ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}} \right] = \frac {\sigma l}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} \left(1 - \frac {x}{\sqrt {x ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}}\right), \quad x > l / 2\tag{12}
$$

在轴线上 x<-l/2 处，两圆面电荷产生的电势为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} U & = U _ {+} + U _ {-} \\ & = \frac {\sigma}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} \left[ \sqrt {(x - l / 2) ^ {2} + R ^ {2}} + x - l / 2 \right] + \frac {- \sigma}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} \left[ \sqrt {(x + l / 2) ^ {2} + R ^ {2}} - x + l / 2 \right] \\ & = \frac {\sigma}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} \left[ \sqrt {(x - l / 2) ^ {2} + R ^ {2}} - \sqrt {(x + l / 2) ^ {2} + R ^ {2}} - l \right] \\ & \approx - \frac {\sigma l}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} \left[ 1 + \frac {x}{\sqrt {x ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}} \right], \quad x <   - l / 2 \end{array}\tag{13}
$$

注意,式(13)因 x<0, 故与式(12)仅差一负号. 这一点从对称性的角度考虑, 也应如此. 在两圆面之间, 轴线上的电势为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} U & = U _ {-} + U _ {-} \\ & = \frac {\sigma}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} \left[ \sqrt {(x - l / 2) ^ {2} + R ^ {2}} + (x - l / 2) \right] + \frac {- \sigma}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} \left[ \sqrt {(x + l / 2) ^ {2} + R ^ {2}} - (x + l / 2) \right] \\ & = \frac {\sigma}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} \left[ \sqrt {(x - l / 2) ^ {2} + R ^ {2}} - \sqrt {(x + l / 2) ^ {2} + R ^ {2}} + 2 x \right] \\ & \approx \frac {\sigma}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} \left[ - \frac {x l}{\sqrt {x ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}} + 2 x \right] \\ & = \frac {\sigma x}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} \left[ 2 - \frac {l}{\sqrt {x ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}} \right] \end{array}\tag{14}
$$

因 $l \ll R$ ，故得

$$
U \approx \frac {\alpha x}{\varepsilon_ {0}}, \quad l / 2 > x > - l / 2\tag{15}
$$

【讨论】也可以先求出电势 U，再由

$$
\boldsymbol {E} = - \boldsymbol {\nabla} U\tag{16}
$$

求电场强度 E, 如下: 由式(12)得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \boldsymbol {E} = - \frac {\partial U}{\partial x} \boldsymbol {e} _ {x} = - \frac {\partial}{\partial x} \frac {\sigma l}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} \left(1 - \frac {x}{\sqrt {x ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}}\right) \boldsymbol {e} _ {x} \\ = \frac {\sigma l R ^ {2}}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {\boldsymbol {e} _ {x}}{(x ^ {2} + R ^ {2}) ^ {3 / 2}}, \quad x > l / 2 \end{array}\tag{17}
$$

由式(13)得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \boldsymbol {E} & = - \frac {\partial U}{\partial x} \boldsymbol {e} _ {x} = \frac {\partial}{\partial x} \frac {\sigma l}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} \left(1 + \frac {x}{\sqrt {x ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}}\right) \\ & = \frac {\sigma l R ^ {2}}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {\boldsymbol {e} _ {x}}{(x ^ {2} + R ^ {2}) ^ {3 / 2}}, x <   - l / 2 \end{array}\tag{18}
$$

由式(15)得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \boldsymbol {E} & = - \frac {\partial U}{\partial x} \boldsymbol {e} _ {x} = - \frac {\partial}{\partial x} \left(\frac {\sigma x}{\varepsilon_ {0}}\right) \boldsymbol {e} _ {x} \\ & = - \frac {\sigma}{\varepsilon_ {0}} \boldsymbol {e} _ {x}, l / 2 > x > - l / 2 \end{array}\tag{19}
$$

1.4.48 电荷均匀分布在半径为 R 的圆面上, 电荷量的面密度为 $\sigma$ . 试求这

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-24/e93caaac-7fa3-4e02-8383-9e78eb19a388/8603158df956fd18815dc292ded96399c2a0394eef861b55e20e049a92f1ca84.jpg)



图1.4.48


圆面边缘的电势.

【解】以圆面边缘上的一点 O 为原点,通过圆心的直径 OP 为极轴,取平面极坐标系,使圆面在平面内,如图 1.4.48 所示,则圆的方程为

$$
\rho = 2 R \cos \theta\tag{1}
$$

式中 $\rho$ 为极径, $\theta$ 为极角. 以 $O$ 为心, $r$ 为半径, 在圆面上取一宽为 $\mathrm{d}r$ 的弧带 $ABC$ . 这弧带上面积元 $\mathrm{d}s = (\mathrm{d}r)(\mathrm{r}\mathrm{d}\theta) - r\mathrm{d}r\mathrm{d}\theta$ 上的电荷量为

$$
\mathrm{d} q = \sigma \mathrm{d} s = \sigma r \mathrm{d} r \mathrm{d} \theta\tag{2}
$$

这电荷在 O 点产生的电势为

$$
\mathrm{d} U = \frac {\mathrm{d} q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r} = \frac {\sigma}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \mathrm{d} r \mathrm{d} \theta\tag{3}
$$

利用对称性,积分便得

$$
\begin{array}{l} U = 2 \cdot \frac {\sigma}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \int_ {0} ^ {2 R} \mathrm{d} r \int_ {0} ^ {\theta_ {m}} \mathrm{d} \theta \\ = \frac {\sigma}{2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \int_ {0} ^ {2 R} \arccos \left(\frac {r}{2 R}\right) \mathrm{d} r \\ = \frac {\sigma R}{\pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \left[ \frac {r}{2 R} \arccos \left(\frac {r}{2 R}\right) - \sqrt {1 - \left(\frac {r}{2 R}\right) ^ {2}} \right] _ {r = 0} ^ {r - 2 R} \\ = \frac {\sigma R}{\pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \end{array}\tag{4}
$$

其中利用了积分公式

$$
\int \arccos x \mathrm{d} x = x \arccos x - \sqrt {1 - x ^ {2}}\tag{5}
$$

1.4.49 电荷量 q 均匀地分布在半径为 R 的球面上。(1)试求离球心为 r 处的电势 U，并由 U 求电场强度 E；(2)试求离球心为 r 处的电场强度 E，并由 E 求电势 U；(3)以 r 为横坐标，U 为纵坐标，画出 U-r 曲线。

【解】（1）设 $P$ 是离球心为 $r$ 处的一点，当 $r < R$ 时， $P$ 在球内，如图1.4.49(1)所示。以 $P$ 到球心 $O$ 的联线所在的直径为轴线，在球面上取一个宽为 $R\mathrm{d}\theta$ 的环带，环带上的电荷量为

$$
\mathrm{d} q = \frac {q}{4 \pi R ^ {2}} \cdot 2 \pi R ^ {2} \sin \theta \mathrm{d} \theta = \frac {q}{2} \sin \theta \mathrm{d} \theta\tag{1}
$$

这电荷在 P 点产生的电势为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \mathrm{d} U & = \frac {\mathrm{d} q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} s} = \frac {q}{8 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {\sin \theta \mathrm{d} \theta}{\sqrt {(R \sin \theta) ^ {2} + (R \cos \theta + r) ^ {2}}} \\ & = \frac {q}{8 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {\sin \theta \mathrm{d} \theta}{\sqrt {R ^ {2} + r ^ {2} + 2 R r \cos \theta}} \end{array}\tag{2}
$$

对 $\theta$ 积分便得整个球面电荷在 P 点产生的电势为

$$
\begin{array}{l} U = \frac {q}{8 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \int_ {0} ^ {\pi} \frac {\sin \theta \mathrm{d} \theta}{\sqrt {R ^ {2} + r ^ {2} + 2 R r \cos \theta}} \\ = - \frac {q}{8 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} R r} \sqrt {R ^ {2} + r ^ {2} + 2 R r \cos \theta} \Bigg | _ {\theta = 0} ^ {\theta = \pi} \\ = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} R}, r <   R \end{array}
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-24/e93caaac-7fa3-4e02-8383-9e78eb19a388/a534e58ea2c12a62b2cd101a20ee146b2ee93abe06d098df84bd8c99ee63dcb6.jpg)


(3) 


图1.4.49(1)


可见球内是一个等势体. 由此得球内的电场强度为

$$
E = - \nabla U = - \nabla \left(\frac {q}{4 \pi \epsilon_ {0} R}\right) = 0\tag{4}
$$

当 $r > R$ 时， $P$ 点在球外，如图1.4.49(2)所示。这时，球面上以 $O$ 到 $P$ 的联线为轴线的环带电荷在 $P$ 点产生的电势仍由式(2)表示。对 $\theta$ 积分便得整个球面电荷在 $P$ 点产生的电势为

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-24/e93caaac-7fa3-4e02-8383-9e78eb19a388/545fe4a03c11686d2a1c5465fd165c98593677766b6b9f7063d3b503736a7233.jpg)



图1.4.49(2)


$$
\begin{array}{l} U = \frac {q}{8 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \int_ {0} ^ {\pi} \frac {\sin \theta \mathrm{d} \theta}{\sqrt {R ^ {2} + r ^ {2} + 2 R r \cos \theta}} \\ = - \frac {q}{8 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} R r} \sqrt {R ^ {2} + r ^ {2}} + 2 R r \cos \theta \Bigg | _ {\theta = 0} ^ {\theta - \pi} \\ = \frac {q}{8 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} R r} \left[ \sqrt {(R + r) ^ {2}} - \sqrt {(r - R) ^ {2}} \right] \\ = \frac {q}{8 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} R r} [ R + r - (r - R) ] \\ = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r}, r > R \end{array}\tag{5}
$$

由此得球外的电场强度为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \boldsymbol {E} & = - \nabla U = - \frac {\partial U}{\partial r} \boldsymbol {e} _ {r} = - \frac {q \boldsymbol {e} _ {r}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} r} \frac {1}{r} \\ & = \frac {q \boldsymbol {e} _ {r}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r ^ {2}} = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {\boldsymbol {r}}{r ^ {3}}, r > R \end{array}\tag{6}
$$

式中 $r = re_{r} = OP$ .

(2) 先求电场强度 E，再由 E 求电势 U。以球心为心、r 为半径作球面（高斯面）S，由对称性和高斯定理得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \iint_ {S} \boldsymbol {E} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {S} & = \iint_ {S} E \mathrm{d} S = E \iint_ {S} \mathrm{d} S \\ & = E \cdot 4 \pi r ^ {2} = \frac {q _ {i}}{\epsilon_ {0}} \end{array}\tag{7}
$$

式中 $q_{i}$ 是 $S$ 所包住的电荷量的代数和. 当 $r < R$ 时, $S$ 在电荷球面内, 故 $q_{i} = 0$ ; 当 $r > R$ 时, $S$ 在电荷球面外, $q_{i} = q$ . 于是得所求的电场强度为

$$
\boldsymbol {E} = 0, r <   R\tag{8}
$$

$$
\boldsymbol {E} = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {\boldsymbol {r}}{r ^ {3}}, r > R\tag{9}
$$

由 E 求电势 U 得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} U & = \int_ {r} ^ {\infty} \boldsymbol {E} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {l} = \int_ {r} ^ {R} \boldsymbol {E} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {l} + \int_ {R} ^ {\infty} \boldsymbol {E} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {l} \\ & = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \int_ {R} ^ {\infty} \frac {\boldsymbol {r} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {r}}{r ^ {3}} = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} R}, r <   R \\ U & = \int_ {r} ^ {\infty} \boldsymbol {E} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {l} = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \int_ {r} ^ {\infty} \frac {\boldsymbol {r} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {r}}{r ^ {3}} \\ & = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r}, r > R \end{array}\tag{10}
$$

(11) 

(3) 按式(3)或式(10)和式(5)或式(11)画出的 U-r 图如图 1.4.49(3) 所示.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-24/e93caaac-7fa3-4e02-8383-9e78eb19a388/33a8ca842166adfdea6e987a2b72f4bbdd1da2abf8550b9919755b47d6086514.jpg)



图1.4.49(3)


【讨论】一、在式(5)的计算过程中，我们取 $\sqrt{R^2 + r^2 - 2Rr} = r - R$ ，而不取 $R - r$ ，这是因为，式(5)中 $\sqrt{R^2 + r^2 + 2Rr\cos\theta} = s$ ，是球面上环带到 $P$ 点的距离，故 $s > 0$ ，所以根号应取正值.

二、由前面的计算可以看出，由于对称性，本题以先用高斯定理求 E，再由 E 求 U 比较简单.

1.4.50 电荷均匀地分布在半径为 R 的球冠(球缺)面上，电荷量的面密度为 $\sigma$ ，球冠边缘对球心 O 的张角为 $2\theta$ ，如图 1.4.50 所示。试求球心 O 的电势。

【解】以球冠的轴线为轴线，在球冠上取宽 $R\mathrm{d}\alpha$ 的环带，这环带上的电荷在球心 $O$ 产生的电势为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} U & = \int_ {0} ^ {\theta} \sigma \cdot \frac {2 \pi R ^ {2} \sin \alpha d \alpha}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} R} = \frac {\sigma R}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} \int_ {0} ^ {\theta} \sin \alpha d \alpha \\ & = \frac {\sigma R}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} (1 - \cos \theta) = \frac {\sigma R}{\varepsilon_ {0}} \sin^ {2} \frac {\theta}{2} \end{array}
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-24/e93caaac-7fa3-4e02-8383-9e78eb19a388/4f2f449b326b2dbf43bebad06fbd5140a23989e2c5301903f37e5692bda98e77.jpg)


(1) 


图1.4.50


若给定球冠面上的电荷量为 q，则不论 q 在球冠面上如何分布，球心的电势总是

$$
U = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} R}\tag{2}
$$

1.4.51 电荷量 q 均匀地分布在半径为 R 的半球面上. 试论证: 这半球的底面上每一点的电势都是 $\frac{q}{4\pi\varepsilon_{0}R}$ .

【论证】对于均匀分布的球面电荷，由对称性和高斯定理得出，球内处处电势相等。设想这球面分为两个半球面，由对称性可知，每个半球面上的电荷在它们的公共底面上任何两点 $A, B$ 产生的电势 $U_A$ 和 $U_B$ 必定相等。因为若 $U_A \neq U_B$ ，则两个半球面合起来成为一个球面时， $A, B$ 两点的电势便不可能相等。因此，我们得出结论：每个半球面上的电荷在其底面上任何一点所产生的电势都相等。由于半球面上的电荷量 $q$ 在球心产生的电势为 $\frac{q}{4\pi\varepsilon_0R}$ ，故得半球底面上每一点的电势都是 $\frac{q}{4\pi\varepsilon_0R}$ 。

1.4.52 电荷均匀分布在半球面上,你能否用对称性和叠加原理论证,这半球的底面上每点的电场强度都与底面垂直?

【论证】根据前面1.4.51题的结论，电荷均匀分布在半球面上，这半球面的底面便是一个等势面。因为电场强度垂直于等势面，所以均匀带电的半球面的底面上每一点，电场强度都与底面垂直。

1.4.53 半径分别为 $R_{1}$ 和 $R_{2}$ 的两个半球面都均匀带电，电荷量的面密度分别为 $\sigma_{1}$ 和 $\sigma_{2}$ ; 这两个半球面的底面重合，球心也重合，如图 1.4.53 所示. 试求公

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-24/e93caaac-7fa3-4e02-8383-9e78eb19a388/2f19f4a99f12dfd936ce84f7b21a47007cccb0d7bda6cf8cc4657200651c19a7.jpg)



图1.4.53


共底面上离球心为 r 处的电势.

【解】由前面1.4.51题的结果,每个均匀带电半球面的底面都是等势面,其电势为

$$
U = \frac {q}{4 \pi \epsilon_ {0} R} = \frac {\sigma \cdot 2 \pi R ^ {2}}{4 \pi \epsilon_ {0} R} = \frac {\sigma R}{2 \epsilon_ {0}}\tag{1}
$$

故两个半球面电荷 $\sigma_{1}$ 和 $\sigma_{2}$ 在它们的底面上产生的电势分别为

$$
U _ {1} = \frac {\sigma_ {1} R _ {1}}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}}, \quad U _ {2} = \frac {\sigma_ {2} R _ {2}}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}}\tag{2}
$$

根据电势叠加原理,在它们的公共底面上离球心为 r 处,电势为

$$
U = U _ {1} + U _ {2} = \frac {1}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} (\sigma_ {1} R _ {1} + \sigma_ {2} R _ {2}), r <   R _ {2}\tag{3}
$$

当 $r > R_{2}$ 时，根据对称性， $\sigma_{2}$ 半球面产生的电势为

$$
U _ {2} = \frac {q _ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r} = \frac {\sigma_ {2} \cdot 2 \pi R _ {2} ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r} = \frac {\sigma_ {2} R _ {2} ^ {2}}{2 \varepsilon_ {0} r}\tag{4}
$$

于是得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} U & = U _ {1} + U _ {2} = \frac {\sigma_ {1} R _ {1}}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} + \frac {\sigma_ {2} R _ {2} ^ {2}}{2 \varepsilon_ {0} r} \\ & = \frac {1}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} \left(\sigma_ {1} R _ {1} + \frac {\sigma_ {2} R _ {2} ^ {2}}{r}\right), R _ {2} <   r <   R _ {1} \end{array}\tag{5}
$$

1.4.54 电荷均匀分布在边长为 a 的正方形平面上, 电荷量的面密度为 $\sigma$ . 试求这正方形中心的电势.

【解】以正方形的中心 $O$ 为原点，正方形的平面为 $x - y$ 平面，取笛卡儿坐标系，使 $x, y$ 轴各平行于两边，如图1.4.54所示。由图可见，正方形由 $x, y$ 轴和对角线分成八个相等的三角形；由对称性可知，每一个三角形上的电荷在中心 $O$ 产生的电势相等，因此，只须计算其中一个三角形，如 $OAB$ ，所产生的电势即可。

由图可见， $x, y$ 处的面积元 $\mathrm{d}S = \mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}y$ 上电荷量为 $\mathrm{d}q = \sigma \mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}y$ ，它在 $O$ 点产生的电势为

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-24/e93caaac-7fa3-4e02-8383-9e78eb19a388/75f98e3c20092e61bfeb619430478c8dfe2a569c5c050ef395492877b7431233.jpg)



图1.4.54


$$
\mathrm{d} U _ {1} = \frac {\sigma \mathrm{d} x \mathrm{d} y}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} \sqrt {x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}}}\tag{1}
$$

所以

$$
\begin{array}{l} U _ {1} = \frac {\sigma}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \int_ {0} ^ {a / 2} \int_ {0} ^ {x} \frac {\mathrm{d} x \mathrm{d} y}{\sqrt {x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}}} = \frac {\sigma}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \int_ {0} ^ {a / 2} \left[ \int_ {0} ^ {x} \frac {\mathrm{d} y}{\sqrt {x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}}} \right] \mathrm{d} x \\ = \frac {\sigma}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \int_ {0} ^ {a / 2} \left[ \ln (y + \sqrt {x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}}) \right] _ {y = 0} ^ {y = x} \mathrm{d} x \\ = \frac {\sigma}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \int_ {0} ^ {a / 2} \ln \left(\frac {x + \sqrt {x ^ {2} + x ^ {2}}}{x}\right) \mathrm{d} x \\ = \frac {\sigma}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \ln (1 + \sqrt {2}) \int_ {0} ^ {a / 2} \mathrm{d} x \\ = \frac {\ln (1 + \sqrt {2})}{8 \pi} \frac {\sigma a}{\varepsilon_ {0}} \end{array}\tag{2}
$$

于是得整个正方形上的电荷在中心产生的电势为

$$
U = 8 U _ {1} = \frac {\ln (1 + \sqrt {2})}{\pi} \frac {\sigma a}{\varepsilon_ {0}}\tag{3}
$$

1.4.55 一球壳体的内外半径分别为 $R_{1}$ 和 $R_{2}$ , 电荷均匀地分布在壳体内, 电荷量密度为 $\rho$ , 如图 1.4.55 所示. (1) 试求离球心为 $r$ 处的电势 $U$ ; (2) 以 $r$ 为横坐标, $U$ 为纵坐标, 画出 $U - r$ 曲线.

【解】（1）先求电场强度 E，再由 E 求电势 U。根据对称性和高斯定理，以球心为心、r 为半径作球面（高斯面）S，便有

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-24/e93caaac-7fa3-4e02-8383-9e78eb19a388/2154ac27a2a646736f08598e5dd62d57e165e8f2140350531cd59ab868e3eada.jpg)


$$
\oiint_ {S} \boldsymbol {E} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {S} = E \cdot 4 \pi r ^ {2} = \frac {q _ {i}}{\varepsilon_ {0}}\tag{1}
$$

图1.4.55

式中 $q_{i}$ 是S所包住的电荷量的代数和，其值如下：

$$
r <   R _ {1}: \quad q _ {i} = 0\tag{2}
$$

$$
R _ {1} <   r <   R _ {2}: \quad q _ {i} = \frac {4 \pi}{3} (r ^ {3} - R _ {1} ^ {3}) \rho\tag{3}
$$

$$
r > R _ {2}; \quad q _ {i} = \frac {4 \pi}{3} (R _ {2} ^ {3} - R _ {1} ^ {3}) \rho\tag{4}
$$

代入式(1)便得

$$
\boldsymbol {E} _ {1} = 0, \quad r <   R _ {1}\tag{5}
$$

$$
\boldsymbol {E} _ {2} = \frac {\rho}{3 \varepsilon_ {0}} \left(1 - \frac {R _ {1} ^ {3}}{r ^ {3}}\right) \boldsymbol {r}, \quad R _ {1} <   r <   R _ {2}\tag{6}
$$

$$
\boldsymbol {E} _ {3} = \frac {\rho}{3 \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {R _ {2} ^ {3} - R _ {1} ^ {3}}{r ^ {3}} \boldsymbol {r}, \quad r > R _ {2}\tag{7}
$$

由 $\pmb{E}$ 求电势如下：

$$
\begin{array}{r l} U _ {3} & = \int_ {r} ^ {\infty} E _ {3} \cdot \mathrm{d} r = \frac {\rho (R _ {2} ^ {3} - R _ {1} ^ {3})}{3 \epsilon_ {0}} \int_ {r} ^ {\infty} \frac {r \cdot \mathrm{d} r}{r ^ {3}} \\ & = \frac {\rho}{3 \epsilon_ {0}} \frac {R _ {2} ^ {3} - R _ {1} ^ {3}}{r}, r > R _ {2} \end{array}\tag{8}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} U _ {2} = \int_ {r} ^ {\infty} \boldsymbol {E} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {r} = \int_ {r} ^ {R _ {2}} \boldsymbol {E} _ {2} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {r} + \int_ {R _ {2}} ^ {\infty} E _ {3} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {r} \\ = \frac {\rho}{3 \varepsilon_ {0}} \int_ {r} ^ {R _ {2}} \left(1 - \frac {R _ {1} ^ {3}}{r ^ {3}}\right) \boldsymbol {r} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {r} + \frac {\rho}{3 \varepsilon_ {0}} (R _ {2} ^ {3} - R _ {1} ^ {3}) \int_ {R _ {2}} ^ {\infty} \frac {\boldsymbol {r} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {r}}{r ^ {3}} \\ = \frac {\rho}{3 \varepsilon_ {0}} \left(\frac {1}{2} r ^ {2} + \frac {R _ {1} ^ {3}}{r}\right) \Bigg | _ {r} ^ {R _ {2}} + \frac {\rho}{3 \varepsilon_ {0}} (R _ {2} ^ {3} - R _ {1} ^ {3}) \left(- \frac {1}{r}\right) _ {R _ {2}} ^ {\infty} \\ = \frac {\rho}{6 \varepsilon_ {0}} \left(3 R _ {2} ^ {2} - r ^ {2} - 2 \frac {R _ {1} ^ {3}}{r}\right), R _ {1} <   r <   R _ {2} \\ U _ {1} = \int_ {r} ^ {\infty} \boldsymbol {E} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {r} = \int_ {r} ^ {R _ {1}} \boldsymbol {E} _ {1} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {r} + \int_ {R _ {1}} ^ {R _ {2}} \boldsymbol {E} _ {2} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {r} + \int_ {R _ {2}} ^ {\infty} \boldsymbol {E} _ {3} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {r} \\ = \frac {\rho}{3 \varepsilon_ {0}} \int_ {R _ {1}} ^ {R _ {2}} \left(1 - \frac {R _ {1} ^ {3}}{r ^ {3}}\right) \boldsymbol {r} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {r} + \frac {\rho}{3 \varepsilon_ {0}} (R _ {2} ^ {3} - R _ {1} ^ {3}) \int_ {R _ {2}} ^ {\in \infty} \frac {\boldsymbol {r} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {r}}{r ^ {3}} \\ = \frac {\rho}{3 \varepsilon_ {0}} \left(\frac {1}{2} r ^ {2} + \frac {R _ {1} ^ {3}}{r}\right) _ {R _ {1}} ^ {R _ {2}} + \frac {\rho}{3 \varepsilon_ {0}} (R _ {2} ^ {3} - R _ {1} ^ {3}) (- \frac {1}{r}) _ {R _ {2}} ^ {\infty} \\ = \frac {\rho}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} (R _ {2} ^ {2} - R _ {1} ^ {2}), r <   R _ {1} \end{array}\tag{9}
$$

(10) 

(2)根据式(8)、(9)、(10)画出的 $U - r$ 曲线如图1.4.55(1)所示.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-24/e93caaac-7fa3-4e02-8383-9e78eb19a388/2d5a94aea15a1e04c0e6e041ae1f3c14fe55f4cc164d113f7bd348d922c8962c.jpg)



图1.4.55(1)


【讨论】本题也可以用公式

$$
U = \frac {1}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \int_ {V} \frac {\rho \mathrm{d} V}{s}
$$

求电势,式中 s 是 $\rho dV$ 到场点的距离.但这样做,计算要复杂些.

1.4.56 电荷量 q 均匀地分布在半径为 R 的球体内。(1)试求离球心为 r 处的电势 U; (2)以 r 为横坐标, U 为纵坐标, 画出 U-r 曲线.

【解】（1）本题先求电场强度 E，再由 E 求 U 比较容易。根据对称性和高斯定理，以球心为心、r 为半径作球面（高斯面）S，便有

$$
\oiint_ {S} \boldsymbol {E} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {S} = E \cdot 4 \pi r ^ {2} = \frac {q _ {i}}{\varepsilon_ {0}}\tag{1}
$$

式中 $q_{i}$ 是 S 所包电荷的代数和, 其值如下:

$$
r \leqslant R: q _ {i} = \frac {4 \pi}{3} r ^ {3} \rho = \frac {4 \pi}{3} r ^ {3} \cdot \frac {3 q}{4 \pi R ^ {3}} = \frac {q}{R ^ {3}} r ^ {3}\tag{2}
$$

$$
r \geqslant R: q _ {i} = q\tag{3}
$$

代入式(1)便得

$$
\boldsymbol {E} _ {1} = \frac {\rho}{3 \varepsilon_ {0}} \boldsymbol {r} = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} R ^ {3}} \boldsymbol {r}, r \leqslant R\tag{4}
$$

$$
\boldsymbol {E} _ {2} = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r ^ {3}} \boldsymbol {r}, r \geqslant R\tag{5}
$$

由 $\pmb{E}$ 求电势如下：

$$
\begin{array}{r l} U _ {2} & = \int_ {r} ^ {\infty} \boldsymbol {E} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {r} = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \int_ {r} ^ {\infty} \frac {\boldsymbol {r} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {r}}{r ^ {3}} = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r}, r \geqslant R \\ U _ {1} & = \int_ {r} ^ {\infty} \boldsymbol {E} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {r} = \int_ {r} ^ {R} \boldsymbol {E} _ {1} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {r} + \int_ {R} ^ {\infty} \boldsymbol {E} _ {2} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {r} \\ & = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} R ^ {3}} \int_ {r} ^ {R} \boldsymbol {r} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {r} + \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \int_ {R} ^ {\infty} \frac {\boldsymbol {r} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {r}}{r ^ {3}} \\ & = \frac {q}{8 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} R} \left(3 - \frac {r ^ {2}}{R ^ {2}}\right), r \leqslant R \end{array}\tag{6}
$$

(7) 

(2)根据式(6)、(7)画出的 U-r 曲线,如图 1.4.56 所示.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-24/e93caaac-7fa3-4e02-8383-9e78eb19a388/79d62c524b116b10b5377311e157648cdfef670bc8983d57ffc00af1cdd6b276.jpg)



图1.4.56


【讨论】在前面1.4.55题中，令 $R_{1}=0,R_{2}=R,\rho=\frac{3q}{4\pi R^{3}}$ ，即为本题.

1.4.57 氢原子处在基态时,核外电荷分布如下:在距离核为 r 处,电荷量密度为 $\rho(r)=-\frac{q}{\pi a^{3}}\mathrm{e}^{-\frac{2r}{a}}$ ,式中 q 是电子电荷量的大小,a 是玻尔半径.试求 r 处(1)核外电荷所产生的电势;(2)所有电荷产生的电势.

【解】（1）以氢核 O 为心、R 和 $R + dR$ 为半径作一球壳，如图 1.4.57 所示，P 点到 O 的距离为 r。球壳上的体积元

$$
\mathrm{d} V = 2 \pi R ^ {2} \sin \theta \mathrm{d} R \mathrm{d} \theta\tag{1}
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-24/e93caaac-7fa3-4e02-8383-9e78eb19a388/5387e6d2a3d3bf00d25f20d3b85fd254168760962ed046ed55d76c02a6df2270.jpg)



图1.4.57


内的电荷量为

$$
\mathrm{d} q = \rho (R) \mathrm{d} V = 2 \pi \rho (R) R ^ {2} \sin \theta \mathrm{d} R \mathrm{d} \theta\tag{2}
$$

它在 $P$ 点产生的电势为

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} \sqrt {R ^ {2} + r ^ {2} - 2 R r \cos \theta}} = \frac {1}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {\rho (R) R ^ {2} \sin \theta \mathrm{d} R \mathrm{d} \theta}{\sqrt {R ^ {2} + r ^ {2} - 2 R r \cos \theta}}\tag{3}
$$

对 $\theta$ 积分得

$$
\mathrm{d} U _ {c} = \frac {1}{2 \varepsilon_ {0}} \rho (R) R ^ {2} \mathrm{d} R \int_ {0} ^ {\pi} \frac {\sin \theta \mathrm{d} \theta}{\sqrt {R ^ {2} + r ^ {2} - 2 R r \cos \theta}}\tag{4}
$$

式中积分

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \int_ {0} ^ {\pi} \frac {\sin \theta \mathrm{d} \theta}{\sqrt {R ^ {2} + r ^ {2} - 2 R r \cos \theta}} & = \frac {1}{R r} \sqrt {R ^ {2} + r ^ {2} - 2 R r \cos \theta} \Bigg | _ {0} ^ {\pi} \\ & = \left\{\frac {1}{R r} (2 R) = \frac {2}{r}, R <   r \right\} \\ & = \left\{\frac {1}{R r} (2 r) - \frac {2}{R}, R > r \right\} \end{array}\tag{5}
$$

代入式(4)积分,便得核外电荷在 r 处产生的电势为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} U _ {c} & = \frac {1}{\varepsilon_ {0} r} \int_ {0} ^ {r} \rho (R) R ^ {2} \mathrm{d} R + \frac {1}{\varepsilon_ {0}} \int_ {r} ^ {\infty} \rho (R) R \mathrm{d} R \\ & = - \frac {q}{\pi \varepsilon_ {0} a ^ {3} r} \int_ {0} ^ {r} \mathrm{e} ^ {- \frac {2 R}{a}} R ^ {2} \mathrm{d} R - \frac {q}{\pi \varepsilon_ {0} a ^ {3}} \int_ {r} ^ {\infty} \mathrm{e} ^ {- \frac {2 R}{a}} R \mathrm{d} R \\ & = - \frac {q}{\pi \varepsilon_ {0} a ^ {3} r} \left[ - \frac {a ^ {3}}{4} \left(2 \frac {r ^ {2}}{a ^ {2}} + 2 \frac {r}{a} + 1\right) \mathrm{e} ^ {- \frac {2 r}{a}} + \frac {a ^ {3}}{4} \right] \\ & - \frac {q}{\pi \varepsilon_ {0} a ^ {3}} \left[ \frac {a ^ {2}}{4} \left(2 \frac {r}{a} + 1\right) \mathrm{e} ^ {- \frac {2 r}{a}} \right] \end{array}
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-24/e93caaac-7fa3-4e02-8383-9e78eb19a388/3353ae0ba26bb6563ea0a6797cad95234db4e8d25915e4122e9ff5348fc8d8c0.jpg)


$$
= \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \left[ \left(\frac {1}{a} + \frac {1}{r}\right) \mathrm{e} ^ {- \frac {2 r}{a}} - \frac {1}{r} \right]\tag{6}
$$

(2)所有电荷在 r 处产生的电势为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} U & = U _ {c} + \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {1}{r} \\ & = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \left[ \left(\frac {1}{a} + \frac {1}{r}\right) \mathrm{e} ^ {- \frac {2 r}{a}} - \frac {1}{r} \right] + \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {1}{r} \\ & = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \left(\frac {1}{a} + \frac {1}{r}\right) \mathrm{e} ^ {- \frac {2 r}{a}} \end{array}\tag{7}
$$

【别解】由电场强度 E 求电势 根据前面 1.3.16 题的式(5)，核外电荷在 r 处产生的电场强度为

$$
\boldsymbol {E} _ {c} = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \left[ \left(\frac {2}{a ^ {2}} + \frac {2}{a r} + \frac {1}{r ^ {2}}\right) \mathrm{e} ^ {- \frac {2 r}{a}} - \frac {1}{r ^ {2}} \right] \boldsymbol {e} _ {r}\tag{8}
$$

式中 $e_{r}$ 是从 O 指向场点的单位矢量. 由此得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} U _ {c} & = \int_ {r} ^ {\infty} \boldsymbol {E} _ {c} \cdot \mathrm{d} r \\ & = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \int_ {r} ^ {\infty} \left[ \left(\frac {2}{a ^ {2}} + \frac {2}{a r} + \frac {1}{r ^ {2}}\right) \mathrm{e} ^ {- \frac {2 r}{a}} - \frac {1}{r ^ {2}} \right] \mathrm{d} r \\ & = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \left(\frac {1}{a} + \frac {1}{r}\right) \mathrm{e} ^ {- \frac {2 r}{a}} - \frac {1}{r} \end{array}\tag{9}
$$

所有电荷在 r 处产生的电场强度为

$$
\boldsymbol {E} = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \left(\frac {2}{a ^ {2}} + \frac {2}{a r} + \frac {1}{r ^ {2}}\right) \mathrm{e} ^ {- \frac {2 r}{a}} \boldsymbol {e} _ {r}\tag{10}
$$

故得

$$
\begin{array}{l} U = \int_ {r} ^ {\infty} \boldsymbol {E} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {r} = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \int_ {r} ^ {\infty} \left(\frac {2}{a ^ {2}} + \frac {2}{a r} + \frac {1}{r ^ {2}}\right) \mathrm{e} ^ {- \frac {2 r}{a}} \mathrm{d} r \\ = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \left(\frac {1}{a} + \frac {1}{r}\right) \mathrm{e} ^ {- \frac {2 r}{a}} \end{array}\tag{11}
$$

1.4.58 两个均匀带电的无限长直共轴圆筒,内筒半径为 a,沿轴线单位长度的电荷量为 $\lambda$ ,外筒半径为 b,沿轴线单位长度的电荷量为 $-\lambda$ .试求:(1)离轴线为 r 处的电势;(2)两筒的电势差.

【解】（1）本题由电场强度求电势比较容易.以轴线为轴、 $r$ 为半径，作一长为 $l$ 的圆柱面，由对称性和高斯定理得

$$
\oiint_ {S} \boldsymbol {E} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {S} = E \cdot 2 \pi r l = \frac {q _ {i}}{\varepsilon_ {0}}\tag{1}
$$

式中 $q_{i}$ 是 S 所包住电荷量的代数和，其值为

$$
q _ {i} = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} 0, & r <   a \\ l \lambda , & a <   r <   b \\ 0, & r > b \end{array} \right.\tag{2}
$$

于是电场强度为

$$
\pmb {E} _ {1} = 0, r <   a\tag{3}
$$

$$
\boldsymbol {E} _ {2} = \frac {\lambda}{2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {\boldsymbol {r}}{r ^ {2}}, a <   r <   b\tag{4}
$$

$$
\mathbf {E} _ {3} = 0, r > b\tag{5}
$$

所求的电势为

$$
U _ {3} = \int_ {r} ^ {\infty} \boldsymbol {E} _ {3} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {r} = 0, r > b\tag{6}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} U _ {2} & = \int_ {r} ^ {\infty} \boldsymbol {E} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {r} = \int_ {r} ^ {b} \boldsymbol {E} _ {2} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {r} + \int_ {b} ^ {\infty} \boldsymbol {E} _ {3} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {r} \\ & = \frac {\lambda}{2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \int_ {r} ^ {b} \frac {\boldsymbol {r} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {r}}{r ^ {2}} = \frac {\lambda}{2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \int_ {r} ^ {b} \frac {\mathrm{d} r}{r} \\ & = \frac {\lambda}{2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \ln \frac {b}{r}, a <   r <   b \end{array}\tag{7}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} U _ {1} & = \int_ {r} ^ {\infty} \boldsymbol {E} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {r} = \int_ {r} ^ {a} \boldsymbol {E} _ {1} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {r} + \int_ {a} ^ {b} \boldsymbol {E} _ {2} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {r} + \int_ {b} ^ {\infty} \boldsymbol {E} _ {3} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {r} \\ & = \frac {\lambda}{2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \int_ {a} ^ {b} \frac {\mathrm{d} r}{r} = \frac {\lambda}{2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \ln \frac {b}{a}, r <   a \end{array}\tag{8}
$$

(2)两筒的电势差为

$$
U _ {a} - U _ {b} = \int_ {a} ^ {b} \boldsymbol {E} _ {2} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {r} = \frac {\lambda}{2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \int_ {a} ^ {b} \frac {\mathrm{d} r}{r} = \frac {\lambda}{2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \ln \frac {b}{a}\tag{9}
$$

1.4.59 两个长直圆筒共轴,它们的半径分别为1.5cm和3.0cm,两筒面上都均匀带电,沿轴线单位长度的电荷量大小相等而符号相反,已知它们的电势差为5000V.试问何处电场强度最大?其值是多少?

【解】根据对称性和高斯定理,得内筒内和外筒外电场强度均为零,两筒间的电场强度为[参见前面1.4.58题的式(4)]

$$
\boldsymbol {E} = \frac {\lambda \boldsymbol {r}}{2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r ^ {2}}\tag{1}
$$

故知内筒外表面附近的电场强度为最大，

$$
E _ {\max} = \frac {\lambda}{2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} a}\tag{2}
$$

两筒的电势差为[参见1.4.58题的式(9)]

$$
U _ {a} - U _ {b} = \frac {\lambda}{2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \ln \frac {b}{a}\tag{3}
$$

代入式(2)，得电场强度的最大值为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} E _ {\max} & = \frac {U _ {a} - U _ {b}}{a \ln (b / a)} = \frac {5 0 0 0}{1 . 5 \times 1 0 ^ {- 2} \ln (3 . 0 / 1 . 5)} \\ & = 4. 8 \times 1 0 ^ {5} (\mathrm{V/m}) \end{array}\tag{4}
$$

1.4.60 根据电场强度 E 与电势 U 的关系 E = -VU，试由下列三种情况的 U 求相应的 E：(1) 点电荷 q: $U = \frac{q}{4\pi\varepsilon_{0}} \frac{1}{r}$ ; (2) 圆环电荷 q 的轴线上: $U = \frac{q}{4\pi\varepsilon_{0}} \times \frac{1}{\sqrt{r^{2} - R^{2}}}$ ，式中 R 是圆环半径，r 是到环心的距离；(3) 电偶极子 p: $U = \frac{p}{4\pi\varepsilon_{0}} \times \frac{\cos\theta}{r^{2}}$ .

【解】（1）点电荷的电场强度

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \boldsymbol {E} & = - \nabla \left(\frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {1}{r}\right) = - \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \nabla \left(\frac {1}{r}\right) \\ & = - \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {\partial}{\partial r} \left(\frac {1}{r}\right) \boldsymbol {e} _ {r} = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {e _ {r}}{r ^ {2}} \\ & = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {\boldsymbol {r}}{r ^ {3}} \end{array}\tag{1}
$$

(2)圆环电荷轴线上的电场强度

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \boldsymbol {E} & = - \nabla \left(\frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {1}{\sqrt {r ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}}\right) = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \nabla \left(\frac {1}{\sqrt {r ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}}\right) \\ & = - \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {\partial}{\partial r} \left(\frac {1}{\sqrt {r ^ {2} + R ^ {2}}}\right) \boldsymbol {e} _ {r} = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {r}{(r ^ {2} + R ^ {2}) ^ {3 / 2}} \boldsymbol {e} _ {r} \\ & = \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {\boldsymbol {r}}{(r ^ {2} + R ^ {2}) ^ {3 / 2}} \end{array}\tag{2}
$$

(3)电偶极子的电场强度

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \boldsymbol {E} & = - \nabla \left(\frac {p}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {\cos \theta}{r ^ {2}}\right) \\ & = - \frac {p}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \left[ \frac {\partial}{\partial r} \left(\frac {\cos \theta}{r ^ {2}}\right) \boldsymbol {e} _ {r} + \frac {1}{r} \frac {\partial}{\partial \theta} \left(\frac {\cos \theta}{r ^ {2}}\right) \boldsymbol {e} _ {\theta} \right] \\ & = \frac {p}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \left[ \frac {2 \cos \theta}{r ^ {3}} \boldsymbol {e} _ {r} + \frac {\sin \theta}{r ^ {3}} \boldsymbol {e} _ {\theta} \right] \end{array}
$$

$$
= \frac {p}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r ^ {3}} \left[ 2 \cos \theta \boldsymbol {e} _ {r} + \sin \theta \boldsymbol {e} _ {\theta} \right]\tag{3}
$$

利用

$$
\boldsymbol {p} = p \cos \theta \boldsymbol {e} _ {r} - p \sin \theta \boldsymbol {e} _ {\theta}\tag{4}
$$

上式可化为

$$
\boldsymbol {E} = \frac {1}{4 \pi \epsilon_ {0} r ^ {5}} [ 3 (\boldsymbol {p} \cdot \boldsymbol {r}) \boldsymbol {r} - r ^ {2} \boldsymbol {p} ]\tag{5}
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-24/e93caaac-7fa3-4e02-8383-9e78eb19a388/4c904fbe6f4593263e01765871d40946c117111457f3dffc9279e97c951ec135.jpg)


## 第二章 导体和电介质

## § 2.1 导体

2.1.1 一导体球壳内是球形空腔, 空腔的中心有一电荷量为 $q_{2}$ 的点电荷, 球

壳外离球心为 r 处有一电荷量为 $q_{1}$ 的点电荷, 如图 2.1.1 所示. 已知导体球壳上所有电荷量的代数和为零. 试求: (1) $q_{1}$ 作用在 $q_{2}$ 上的力; (2) $q_{2}$ 所受的力.

【解】（1）因电荷之间的相互作用力(库仑力)与其他物质或电荷是否存在无关，故 $q_{1}$ 作用在 $q_{2}$ 上的力为

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-24/e93caaac-7fa3-4e02-8383-9e78eb19a388/40930af430451055faf37a5cec1cddb3374fd506b9bab19d8e53ee9cd21eefbc.jpg)


$$
F _ {2 1} = \frac {1}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {q _ {1} q _ {2}}{r ^ {2}} e _ {1 2}\tag{1}
$$

图2.1.1

式中 $e_{12}$ 是从 $q_{1}$ 到 $q_{2}$ 方向上的单位矢量.

(2) $q_{2}$ 所受的力包括三部分: $q_{1}$ 作用在 $q_{2}$ 上的力 $F_{21}$ , 球壳内表面上的电荷量 $q_{3}$ 作用在 $q_{2}$ 上的力 $F_{23}$ , 和球壳外表面上的电荷量 $q_{4}$ 作用在 $q_{2}$ 上的力 $F_{24}$ . 根据对称性和高斯定理可知, $q_{3} = -q_{2}$ , 它均匀分布在空腔的内表面上, 由于均匀分布的球面电荷在球内产生的电场强度为零, 故 $q_{3}$ 作用在 $q_{2}$ 上的力为

$$
\boldsymbol {F} _ {2 3} = 0\tag{2}
$$

$q_{4}$ 有两部分， $q_{4}=q_{4}^{\prime}+q_{4}^{\prime\prime}$ ，其中 $q_{4}^{\prime}$ 是由于空腔内表面上有 $-q_{2}$ ，而球壳上所有电荷量的代数和为零，故在外表面上出现的电荷， $q_{4}^{\prime}=q_{2}$ ，它均匀分布在外表面上，所以 $q_{4}^{\prime}$ 作用在 $q_{2}$ 上的力为零。 $q_{4}^{\prime\prime}$ 是壳外的 $q_{1}$ 在外表面上引起的感应电荷，虽然 $q_{4}^{\prime\prime}=0$ ，但由于它不是均匀分布在外表面上，故它对 $q_{2}$ 的作用力不为零。这个力可以从如下的考虑得出：导体外表面上感应电荷的分布规律是，它在导体内产生的电场强度，正好与引起它的电荷（此处即 $q_{1}$ ）在导体内产生的电场强度互相抵消，使得导体内的电场强度处处为零。因此， $q_{4}^{\prime\prime}$ 作用在 $q_{2}$ 上的力便等于 $q_{1}$ 作用在 $q_{2}$ 上的力的负值，即

$$
\boldsymbol {F} _ {2 4} = - \boldsymbol {F} _ {2 1}\tag{3}
$$

最后便得 $q_{2}$ 所受的力为

$$
\boldsymbol {F} _ {2} = \boldsymbol {F} _ {2 1} + \boldsymbol {F} _ {2 3} + \boldsymbol {F} _ {2 4} = \boldsymbol {F} _ {2 1} - \boldsymbol {F} _ {2 2} = 0\tag{4}
$$

【讨论】 $q_{1}$ 与 $q_{4}^{\prime\prime}(q_{1}$ 引起的感应电荷)之间的相互作用力可用电像法求得.例如,参见张之翔等,《电动力学》(气象出版社,1988),349页.

2.1.2 一金属球内有两个球形空腔,两空腔中心相距为 a,它们的联线通过球心;在两腔中心各有一个点电荷,电荷量分别为 $q_{1}$ 和 $q_{2}$ . 球外有一电荷量为 q 的点电荷,处在 $q_{2}$ 到 $q_{1}$ 的延长线上,到 $q_{1}$ 的距离为 b,如图 2.1.2 所示. 已知金属球上所有电荷量的代数和为零. 试求金属球上的电荷作用在 $q_{2}$ 上的力.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-24/e93caaac-7fa3-4e02-8383-9e78eb19a388/560781afe4fbac9b140e68ccdc449e7ea26149abf20ddcaa0ab3184dd9ea275e.jpg)


【解】金属球上的电荷包括金属球外表面上的电荷和两腔内表面上的电荷。根据对称性和高斯定理，两腔内表面上的电荷量分别为 $-q_{1}$ 和 $-q_{2}$ ，它们都均匀分布在各自腔内表面上。因此， $-q_{1}$ 作用在 $q_{2}$ 上的力为


图2.1.2


$$
\boldsymbol {F} _ {2 1} = \frac {1}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {q _ {2} (- q _ {1})}{a ^ {2}} \boldsymbol {e} _ {1 2} = - \frac {1}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {q _ {1} q _ {2}}{a ^ {2}} \boldsymbol {e} _ {1 2} (1)
$$

式中 $e_{12}$ 是从 $q_{1}$ 指向 $q_{2}$ 的单位矢量. $-q_{2}$ 由于是均匀分布在球面上，故它作用在里面 $q_{2}$ 上的力为

$$
\boldsymbol {F} _ {2 2} = 0\tag{2}
$$

由于金属球上所有电荷量的代数和为零，故它的外表面上的电荷量便为 $q_{1} + q_{2} + q^{\prime}$ ，其中 $q_{1}$ 和 $q_{2}$ 都均匀分布在外表面上，故作用在 $q_{2}$ 上的力为零. $q^{\prime}$ 是 $q$ 所引起的感应电荷， $q^{\prime}$ 与 $q$ 在导体内产生的电场强度互相抵消，处处为零，故 $q^{\prime}$ 作用在 $q_{2}$ 上的力便等于 $q$ 作用在 $q_{2}$ 上的力的负值，即

$$
\boldsymbol {F} _ {2} ^ {\prime} = - \frac {1}{4 \pi \epsilon_ {0}} \frac {q _ {2} q}{(a + b) ^ {2}} \boldsymbol {e} _ {1 2}\tag{3}
$$

于是得出，金属球上的电荷作用在 $q_{2}$ 上的力为

$$
\boldsymbol {F} _ {2} = \boldsymbol {F} _ {2 1} + \boldsymbol {F} _ {2 2} + \boldsymbol {F} _ {2} ^ {\prime} = - \frac {q _ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \left[ \frac {q _ {1}}{a ^ {2}} + \frac {q}{(a + b) ^ {2}} \right] \boldsymbol {e} _ {1 2}\tag{4}
$$

2.1.3 两金属球壳 A 和 B 中心相距为 r, A 和 B 原来都不带电. 现在 A、B 的中心各放一个点电荷, 电荷量分别为 $q_{1}$ 和 $q_{2}$ , 如图 2.1.3 所示.

(1) 试求 $q_{1}$ 作用在 $q_{2}$ 上的力. $q_{2}$ 有加速度吗?

(2) 去掉金属壳 B, 试求 $q_{1}$ 作用在 $q_{2}$ 上的力. 这时 $q_{2}$ 有加速度吗?

【解】(1) $q_{1}$ 作用在 $q_{2}$ 上的力为

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-24/e93caaac-7fa3-4e02-8383-9e78eb19a388/761f0394125e99beb6d225bc24f3b560e3fbb876a03eaff411676af26e99db8d.jpg)



图2.1.3


$$
\boldsymbol {F} = \frac {1}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {q _ {2} q _ {1}}{r ^ {2}} e _ {1 2}\tag{1}
$$

式中 $e_{12}$ 是从 $q_{1}$ 指向 $q_{2}$ 的单位矢量.

$q_{2}$ 没有加速度. 因为 $A, B$ 上的感应电荷和 $q_{1}$ 一起, 作用在 $q_{2}$ 上的合力为零, 故 $q_{2}$ 的加速度为零.

(2)去掉金属壳 B 后, $q_{1}$ 作用在 $q_{2}$ 上的力仍为

$$
\boldsymbol {F} = \frac {1}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {q _ {2} q _ {1}}{r ^ {2}} e _ {1 2}\tag{2}
$$

这时 $q_{2}$ 没有屏蔽， $q_{1}$ 和 $A$ 上的感应电荷作用在 $q_{2}$ 上的合力一般地不为零，故 $q_{2}$ 有加速度。[关于这时 $q_{2}$ 受力的问题，可参看后面2.1.46题。]

2.1.4 我们通常说，在静电平衡时，导体内部的电场强度处处为零。但是，由于导体是由原子核和电子构成的，而原子核是不连续的，因此，在靠近原子核的地方，必然存在很强的电场。你怎样看待这个矛盾？

【解答】在电磁学里,我们所涉及的量如电荷量密度 $\rho$ 、电场强度 $E$ 、电势 $U$ 等,除特别指明者外,一般都是宏观量,它们都是对宏观小而微观大的区域统计平均的结果。通常说导体内部电场强度处处为零,指的是宏观电场;而“靠近原子核的地方有很强的电场”,则指的是微观电场。一个不带电的导体,在静电平衡时,从微观上看,其中有原子核,核外有电子,显然,各点的电荷量密度和电场强度彼此相差很大;但对于一个宏观小而微观大的区域(例如线度小于 $10^{-5}$ 米和大于 $10^{-8}$ 米的区域)来说,平均起来,则处处电荷量密度 $\rho = 0$ ,电场强度 $E = 0$ 。关于这些,可参看 J.D. 杰克逊著(朱培豫译),《经典电动力学》上册(人民教育出版社,1979),250页。

2.1.5 有人说：“在静电情况下，导体带电有两条规律：(1)电荷只分布在外表面上，内表面上无电荷；(2)电荷量的面密度与该处表面的曲率半径成反比。”你认为他说的这两点对不对？为什么？

【解答】（1）不一定对。在导体里面的空腔里如果有电荷，则空腔的表面（即导体的内表面）上便有电荷，只有在空腔内无电荷时，空腔的表面上才无电荷。

(2)不对. 导体表面某处的曲率半径越小, 则该处电荷量的面密度就越大, 但不一定是反比关系. 还有, 导体表面某处电荷量的面密度不仅与该处的曲率半径有关, 还与周围环境有关, 关系是很复杂的.

2.1.6 一个有厚度的金属球壳带有电荷量 $q$ ，现在它的中心放一个电荷量为 $-q$ 的点电荷，试问：(1)金属球壳上电荷如何分布？(2)这与“导体上的电荷只分布在外表面上”的结论有无矛盾？(3)导体带电时，在什么情况下电荷才完全分布在外表面上？

【解答】（1）由对称性、高斯定理和电荷量守恒得出：球壳的外表面上无电荷，内表面上均匀地分着电荷量为 $-q$ 的电荷.

(2)无矛盾.“导体上的电荷只分布在外表面上”的结论是有条件的,这条件是:导体空腔内无电荷.

(3)导体的空腔内没有电荷.

2.1.7 一导体球壳 $A$ 带有电荷量 $q_{A} = -1 \times 10^{-6}$ 库仑, 导体小球 $B$ 带有电荷量 $q_{B} = 2 \times 10^{-6}$ 库仑. 用丝线吊着小球 $B$ , 经 $A$ 上的小孔放入 $A$ 内. (1) $B$ 不与 $A$ 接触, 令 $A$ 瞬时接地, 如图 2.1.7(1) 所示, 然后断开接地, 再把 $B$ 取出. 问 $A$ 、 $B$ 的带电情况各如何? (2) 如图 2.1.7(2) 所示, $B$ 与 $A$ 的内壁接触, $A$ 不接地, 然后把 $B$ 取出. 问这时 $A$ 、 $B$ 的带电情况各如何?

【解答】(1) A 带的电荷量为 $q_{A}^{\prime} = -q_{B} = -2 \times 10^{-6}$ 库仑. 分布在外表面上; B 带的电荷量

$q_{B}^{\prime} = q_{B} = 2\times 10^{-6}$ 库仑.

(2) A 带的电荷量为 $q_{A}^{\prime\prime}=q_{A}+q_{B}=1\times10^{-6}$ 库仑，分布在外表面上；B 带的电荷量为 $q_{B}^{\prime\prime}=0$ .

(1)

(2)
